============================================================
SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
============================================================

This file is rebuilt from the current Cinerific workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.

The snapshot intentionally ignores:
- .git
- .gradle
- .idea
- app/build
- build
- .kotlin
- .tmp
- .cache
- .DS_Store
- local.properties
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.jks
- *.keystore

Binary image and screenshot files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.

     1	package com.prahlin.cinerific
     2	
     3	import android.app.UiModeManager
     4	import android.content.pm.ActivityInfo
     5	import android.graphics.Color
     6	import android.os.Build
     7	import android.os.Bundle
     8	import android.os.SystemClock
     9	import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
    10	import androidx.activity.SystemBarStyle
    11	import androidx.activity.compose.setContent
    12	import androidx.activity.enableEdgeToEdge
    13	import androidx.core.view.WindowCompat
    14	import com.prahlin.cinerific.ui.CinerificApp
    15	import com.prahlin.cinerific.ui.theme.CinerificTheme
    16	
    17	class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {
    18	    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
    19	        val bootStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
    20	        requestedOrientation = ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_FULL_SENSOR
    21	        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
    22	            getSystemService(UiModeManager::class.java).setApplicationNightMode(
    23	                UiModeManager.MODE_NIGHT_YES
    24	            )
    25	        }
    26	        enableEdgeToEdge(
    27	            statusBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.dark(Color.TRANSPARENT),
    28	            navigationBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.dark(Color.BLACK),
    29	        )
    30	        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
    31	        window.statusBarColor = Color.TRANSPARENT
    32	        window.navigationBarColor = Color.BLACK
    33	        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.P) {
    34	            window.navigationBarDividerColor = Color.BLACK
    35	        }
    36	        WindowCompat.getInsetsController(window, window.decorView).apply {
    37	            isAppearanceLightStatusBars = false
    38	            isAppearanceLightNavigationBars = false
    39	        }
    40	        setContent {
    41	            CinerificTheme {
    42	                CinerificApp(bootStartMillis = bootStartMillis)
    43	            }
    44	        }
    45	    }
    46	}

   122	@Composable
   123	fun CinerificApp(bootStartMillis: Long = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()) {
   124	    val introBootStartMillis by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(bootStartMillis) }
   125	    var showHome by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
   126	    var signedInProfile by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(CinerificProfile.Guest) }
   127	    var selectedLanguage by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(CinerificLanguage.English) }
   128	
   129	    if (showHome) {
   130	        CinerificLocalizedResources(selectedLanguage) {
   131	            CinerificMainExperience(
   132	                signedInProfile = signedInProfile,
   133	                selectedLanguage = selectedLanguage,
   134	                onLanguageSelected = { selectedLanguage = it },
   135	                onSignOut = {
   136	                    signedInProfile = CinerificProfile.Guest
   137	                    showHome = false
   138	                }
   139	            )
   140	        }
   141	    } else {
   142	        AndroidView(
   143	            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
   144	            factory = { context ->
   145	                CinerificIntroView(context).apply {
   146	                    this.bootStartMillis = introBootStartMillis
   147	                    onAvatarSelected = { profile ->
   148	                        signedInProfile = profile
   149	                        showHome = true
   150	                    }
   151	                }
   152	            },
   153	            update = { view ->
   154	                view.bootStartMillis = introBootStartMillis
   155	                view.onAvatarSelected = { profile ->
   156	                    signedInProfile = profile
   157	                    showHome = true
   158	                }
   159	            }
   160	        )
   161	    }
   162	}

   183	@Composable
   184	private fun CinerificMainExperience(
   185	    signedInProfile: CinerificProfile,
   186	    selectedLanguage: CinerificLanguage,
   187	    onLanguageSelected: (CinerificLanguage) -> Unit,
   188	    onSignOut: () -> Unit
   189	) {
   190	    var destination by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(CinerificDestination.Home) }
   191	    var selectedProgramTitle by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("Sink or Swim") }
   192	    var favoriteProgramTitles by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(emptyList<String>()) }
   193	    var userProgramRatings by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(emptyMap<String, Int>()) }
   194	    var catalogRouteDestinationName by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("") }
   195	    var catalogRouteGenreName by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("") }
   196	    var catalogRouteModeName by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("") }
   197	    var favoritesFullPromptRequestId by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }
   198	    var autoLogoutEnabled by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
   199	    var userInitiatedPlaybackActive by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   200	    var lastInteractionMillis by remember { mutableStateOf(SystemClock.uptimeMillis()) }
   201	    val playbackSessionController = remember(userInitiatedPlaybackActive) {
   202	        CinerificPlaybackSessionController(
   203	            isUserInitiatedPlaybackActive = userInitiatedPlaybackActive,
   204	            onUserInitiatedPlaybackStarted = {
   205	                userInitiatedPlaybackActive = true
   206	            },
   207	            finishUserInitiatedPlayback = {
   208	                userInitiatedPlaybackActive = false
   209	                lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   210	            }
   211	        )
   212	    }
   213	    val catalogRoute = remember(
   214	        catalogRouteDestinationName,
   215	        catalogRouteGenreName,
   216	        catalogRouteModeName
   217	    ) {
   218	        cinerificSavedCatalogRoute(
   219	            destinationName = catalogRouteDestinationName,
   220	            genreName = catalogRouteGenreName,
   221	            modeName = catalogRouteModeName
   222	        )
   223	    }
   224	
   225	    fun clearCatalogRoute() {
   226	        catalogRouteDestinationName = ""
   227	        catalogRouteGenreName = ""
   228	        catalogRouteModeName = ""
   229	    }
   230	
   231	    LaunchedEffect(autoLogoutEnabled, userInitiatedPlaybackActive, lastInteractionMillis) {
   232	        if (!autoLogoutEnabled || userInitiatedPlaybackActive) return@LaunchedEffect
   233	        delay(AUTO_LOGOUT_TIMEOUT_MS)
   234	        val inactiveForMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - lastInteractionMillis
   235	        if (
   236	            autoLogoutEnabled &&
   237	            !userInitiatedPlaybackActive &&
   238	            inactiveForMillis >= AUTO_LOGOUT_TIMEOUT_MS
   239	        ) {
   240	            onSignOut()
   241	        }
   242	    }
   243	
   244	    fun showProgramDetails(title: String) {
   245	        clearCatalogRoute()
   246	        selectedProgramTitle = title
   247	        destination = CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails
   248	    }
   249	
   250	    fun showCatalog(route: CinerificCatalogRoute) {
   251	        catalogRouteDestinationName = route.destination.name
   252	        catalogRouteGenreName = route.genre.name
   253	        catalogRouteModeName = route.mode.name
   254	        destination = route.destination
   255	    }
   256	
   257	    fun rateProgram(title: String, rating: Int) {
   258	        userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings + (title to rating.coerceIn(1, 5))
   259	    }
   260	
   261	    fun toggleFavoriteProgram(title: String) {
   262	        if (title in favoriteProgramTitles) {
   263	            favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles - title
   264	            return
   265	        }
   266	
   267	        val isShow = cinerificProgramIsShow(title)
   268	        val filledSlots = cinerificFilledFavoritePlaceholderCount(
   269	            titles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   270	            isShow = isShow
   271	        )
   272	        if (filledSlots >= CINERIFIC_FAVORITES_CAPACITY) {
   273	            favoritesFullPromptRequestId += 1
   274	            return
   275	        }
   276	
   277	        favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles + title
   278	    }
   279	
   280	    CompositionLocalProvider(LocalCinerificPlaybackSessionController provides playbackSessionController) {
   281	        Box(
   282	            modifier = Modifier
   283	                .fillMaxSize()
   284	                .pointerInput(Unit) {
   285	                    awaitPointerEventScope {
   286	                        while (true) {
   287	                            awaitPointerEvent(PointerEventPass.Initial)
   288	                            lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   289	                        }
   290	                    }
   291	                }
   292	        ) {
   293	            when (destination) {
   294	                CinerificDestination.Home -> CinerificHomeScreen(
   295	                    onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   296	                    onCatalogSelected = ::showCatalog,
   297	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   298	                )
   299	                CinerificDestination.Movies,
   300	                CinerificDestination.Shows,
   301	                CinerificDestination.Favorites,
   302	                CinerificDestination.Settings -> CinerificDestinationScreen(
   303	                    destination = destination,
   304	                    signedInProfile = signedInProfile,
   305	                    selectedLanguage = selectedLanguage,
   306	                    onLanguageSelected = onLanguageSelected,
   307	                    autoLogoutEnabled = autoLogoutEnabled,
   308	                    onAutoLogoutEnabledChange = { enabled ->
   309	                        autoLogoutEnabled = enabled
   310	                        lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   311	                    },
   312	                    onSignOut = onSignOut,
   313	                    favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   314	                    onFavoriteToggled = ::toggleFavoriteProgram,
   315	                    userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings,
   316	                    onProgramRated = ::rateProgram,
   317	                    onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   318	                    catalogRoute = catalogRoute?.takeIf { it.destination == destination },
   319	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   320	                )
   321	                CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails -> CinerificProgramDetailsScreen(
   322	                    programTitle = selectedProgramTitle,
   323	                    favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   324	                    onFavoriteToggled = ::toggleFavoriteProgram,
   325	                    userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings,
   326	                    onProgramRated = ::rateProgram,
   327	                    onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   328	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   329	                )
   330	            }
   331	
   332	            CinerificRightSideNavBar(
   333	                currentDestination = destination,
   334	                onDestinationSelected = {
   335	                    clearCatalogRoute()
   336	                    destination = it
   337	                },
   338	                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   339	            )
   340	
   341	            FavoritesFullPromptOverlay(
   342	                requestId = favoritesFullPromptRequestId,
   343	                currentDestination = destination,
   344	                suppressed = userInitiatedPlaybackActive,
   345	                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   346	            )
   347	        }
   348	    }
   349	}
   350	
   351	private fun cinerificSavedCatalogRoute(
   352	    destinationName: String,
   353	    genreName: String,
   354	    modeName: String
   355	): CinerificCatalogRoute? {
   356	    if (destinationName.isBlank() || genreName.isBlank() || modeName.isBlank()) return null
   357	
   358	    val destination = CinerificDestination.values().firstOrNull { it.name == destinationName } ?: return null
   359	    val genre = ViewportGenre.values().firstOrNull { it.name == genreName } ?: return null
   360	    val mode = ViewportMode.values().firstOrNull { it.name == modeName } ?: return null
   361	
   362	    return CinerificCatalogRoute(
   363	        destination = destination,
   364	        genre = genre,
   365	        mode = mode
   366	    )
   367	}
   368	
   369	@Composable
   370	private fun FavoritesFullPromptOverlay(
   371	    requestId: Int,
   372	    currentDestination: CinerificDestination,
   373	    suppressed: Boolean,
   374	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   375	) {
   376	    var visible by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   377	
   378	    LaunchedEffect(requestId, suppressed) {
   379	        if (suppressed) {
   380	            visible = false
   381	            return@LaunchedEffect
   382	        }
   383	        if (requestId == 0) {
   384	            visible = false
   385	            return@LaunchedEffect
   386	        }
   387	        visible = true
   388	        delay(FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_VISIBLE_MS)
   389	        visible = false
   390	    }
   391	
   392	    val alpha by animateFloatAsState(
   393	        targetValue = if (visible) 1f else 0f,
   394	        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = if (visible) 140 else 190),
   395	        label = "favorites-full-prompt-alpha"
   396	    )
   397	    val promptScale by animateFloatAsState(
   398	        targetValue = if (visible) 1f else 0.94f,
   399	        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = if (visible) 140 else 190),
   400	        label = "favorites-full-prompt-scale"
   401	    )
   402	
   403	    if (suppressed || requestId == 0 || (!visible && alpha <= 0.01f)) return
   404	
   405	    BoxWithConstraints(modifier = modifier) {
   406	        val stageScale = maxWidth.value / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH
   407	        val isPortraitDetail = currentDestination == CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails &&
   408	            maxHeight > maxWidth
   409	        val promptY = if (isPortraitDetail) {
   410	            DETAIL_HERO_LOGO_CENTER_Y - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT / 2f
   411	        } else {
   412	            FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_Y
   413	        }
   414	        FavoritesFullPromptBubble(
   415	            stageScale = stageScale,
   416	            modifier = Modifier
   417	                .absoluteOffset(
   418	                    x = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_X * stageScale).dp,
   419	                    y = (promptY * stageScale).dp
   420	                )
   421	                .graphicsLayer {
   422	                    this.alpha = alpha
   423	                    scaleX = promptScale
   424	                    scaleY = promptScale
   425	                    transformOrigin = TransformOrigin(0.5f, 0f)
   426	                }
   427	        )
   428	    }
   429	}
   430	
   431	@Composable
   432	private fun FavoritesFullPromptBubble(
   433	    stageScale: Float,
   434	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   435	) {
   436	    Box(
   437	        modifier = modifier.requiredSize(
   438	            width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH * stageScale).dp,
   439	            height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT * stageScale).dp
   440	        )
   441	    ) {
   442	        Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
   443	            val xScale = size.width / FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH
   444	            val yScale = size.height / FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT
   445	            val pointer = Path().apply {
   446	                moveTo(47.5f * xScale, 0f)
   447	                lineTo(63f * xScale, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale)
   448	                lineTo(32f * xScale, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale)
   449	                close()
   450	            }
   451	            drawPath(pointer, ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent)
   452	            drawRoundRect(
   453	                color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptFill,
   454	                topLeft = Offset(0f, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale),
   455	                size = Size(
   456	                    width = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH * xScale,
   457	                    height = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_HEIGHT * yScale
   458	                ),
   459	                cornerRadius = CornerRadius(
   460	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * xScale,
   461	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * yScale
   462	                )
   463	            )
   464	            drawRoundRect(
   465	                color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent,
   466	                topLeft = Offset(
   467	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * xScale / 2f,
   468	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale +
   469	                        FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * yScale / 2f
   470	                ),
   471	                size = Size(
   472	                    width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE) * xScale,
   473	                    height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_HEIGHT - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE) * yScale
   474	                ),
   475	                cornerRadius = CornerRadius(
   476	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * xScale,
   477	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * yScale
   478	                ),
   479	                style = Stroke(width = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * min(xScale, yScale))
   480	            )
   481	        }
   482	        Text(
   483	            text = "You've\nfilled\nup on\nfavorites!",
   484	            color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent,
   485	            fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
   486	            fontSize = (14f * stageScale).sp,
   487	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
   488	            lineHeight = (21.252f * stageScale).sp,
   489	            textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
   490	            modifier = Modifier
   491	                .absoluteOffset(
   492	                    x = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_X * stageScale).dp,
   493	                    y = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_Y * stageScale).dp
   494	                )
   495	                .requiredSize(
   496	                    width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_WIDTH * stageScale).dp,
   497	                    height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_HEIGHT * stageScale).dp
   498	                )
   499	        )
   500	    }
   501	}
   502	
   503	@Composable
   504	private fun BootIntroFromFigma(progress: Float) {
   505	    val p = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   506	    val images = rememberIntroImages()
   507	    val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
   508	        p,
   509	        SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
   510	        SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
   511	    )
   512	    val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(p, 2500, 3900)
   513	    val background = introBackgroundBrush(
   514	        solidProgress = blackToPurple,
   515	        gradientProgress = purpleToSettle
   516	    )
   517	    val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(p, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
   518	    val logoEntryProgress = bouncySegmentMs(p, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
   519	    val logoFinalProgress = easedSegmentMs(p, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   520	    val avatarSettleAlpha = easedSegmentMs(p, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   521	    val avatarYOffset = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarSettleAlpha)
   522	
   523	    Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
   524	        val stageScale = min(size.width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, size.height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
   525	        val stageLeft = (size.width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f
   526	        val stageTop = (size.height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * stageScale) / 2f
   527	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY(size.width > size.height)
   528	
   529	        drawRect(brush = background, size = size)
   530	
   531	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
   532	            val baseBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 222f, y = 150f, w = 750f, h = 535f)
   533	            val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(
   534	                start = FigmaBounds(x = 447f, y = -417f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   535	                end = baseBounds,
   536	                amount = logoEntryProgress
   537	            )
   538	            val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(
   539	                start = FigmaBounds(x = 453f, y = 1037f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   540	                end = baseBounds,
   541	                amount = logoEntryProgress
   542	            )
   543	            val finalBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 297f, y = 11f, w = 600f, h = 428f)
   544	
   545	            drawFigmaImage(
   546	                image = images.logoSimple,
   547	                bounds = lerpBounds(simpleEntry, finalBounds, logoFinalProgress),
   548	                stageLeft = stageLeft,
   549	                stageTop = stageTop,
   550	                stageScale = stageScale,
   551	                alpha = logoAlpha
   552	            )
   553	            drawFigmaImage(
   554	                image = images.logoEyes,
   555	                bounds = lerpBounds(eyesEntry, finalBounds, logoFinalProgress),
   556	                stageLeft = stageLeft,
   557	                stageTop = stageTop,
   558	                stageScale = stageScale,
   559	                alpha = logoAlpha
   560	            )
   561	        }
   562	
   563	        if (avatarSettleAlpha > 0.01f) {
   564	            val y = avatarYOffset
   565	            drawFigmaImage(images.steveAvatar, FigmaBounds(152f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   566	            drawFigmaImage(images.martinAvatar, FigmaBounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   567	            drawFigmaImage(images.jannyAvatar, FigmaBounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   568	            drawFigmaImage(images.guestAvatar, FigmaBounds(866f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   569	
   570	            drawFigmaImage(images.steveName, FigmaBounds(130f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   571	            drawFigmaImage(images.martinName, FigmaBounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   572	            drawFigmaImage(images.jannyName, FigmaBounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   573	            drawFigmaImage(images.guestName, FigmaBounds(854f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 200f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   574	        }
   575	    }
   576	}

   588

   658	        )
   659	    }

   769	
   770	private fun DrawScope.drawFigmaImage(
   771	    image: ImageBitmap,
   772	    bounds: FigmaBounds,
   773	    stageLeft: Float,
   774	    stageTop: Float,
   775	    stageScale: Float,
   776	    alpha: Float = 1f,
   777	    clipCircle: Boolean = false
   778	) {
   779	    val left = stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale
   780	    val top = stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale
   781	    val width = bounds.w * stageScale
   782	    val height = bounds.h * stageScale
   783	
   784	    if (clipCircle) {
   785	        val clip = Path().apply {
   786	            addOval(Rect(left, top, left + width, top + height))
   787	        }
   788	        clipPath(clip) {
   789	            drawImageBitmap(image, left, top, width, height, alpha)
   790	        }
   791	    } else {
   792	        drawImageBitmap(image, left, top, width, height, alpha)
   793	    }
   794	}
   795	
   796	private fun DrawScope.drawImageBitmap(
   797	    image: ImageBitmap,
   798	    left: Float,
   799	    top: Float,
   800	    width: Float,
   801	    height: Float,
   802	    alpha: Float
   803	) {
   804	    drawImage(
   805	        image = image,
   806	        dstOffset = IntOffset(left.roundToInt(), top.roundToInt()),
   807	        dstSize = IntSize(width.roundToInt(), height.roundToInt()),
   808	        alpha = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   809	    )
   810	}

   812	@Composable
   813	private fun SettlingLogoLayer(
   814	    scale: Float,
   815	    entryProgress: Float,
   816	    finalProgress: Float,
   817	    alpha: Float
   818	) {
   819	    val baseBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 222f, y = 150f, w = 750f, h = 535f)
   820	    val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(
   821	        start = FigmaBounds(x = 447f, y = 1037f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   822	        end = baseBounds,
   823	        amount = entryProgress
   824	    )
   825	    val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(
   826	        start = FigmaBounds(x = 453f, y = -417f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   827	        end = baseBounds,
   828	        amount = entryProgress
   829	    )
   830	    val finalBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 297f, y = 11f, w = 600f, h = 428f)
   831	    val simpleBounds = lerpBounds(simpleEntry, finalBounds, finalProgress)
   832	    val eyesBounds = lerpBounds(eyesEntry, finalBounds, finalProgress)
   833	
   834	    TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
   835	        base = baseBounds,
   836	        target = simpleBounds,
   837	        scale = scale,
   838	        resId = R.drawable.logo_simple_large,
   839	        alpha = alpha
   840	    )
   841	    TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
   842	        base = baseBounds,
   843	        target = eyesBounds,
   844	        scale = scale,
   845	        resId = R.drawable.logo_eyes_large,
   846	        alpha = alpha
   847	    )
   848	}

   850	@Composable
   851	private fun AvatarSelectionLayer(scale: Float, alpha: Float, yOffset: Float) {
   852	    val density = LocalDensity.current.density
   853	    val configuration = LocalConfiguration.current
   854	    val isLandscape = configuration.screenWidthDp > configuration.screenHeightDp
   855	    val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY(isLandscape)
   856	    val accountPromptExtraShiftY = signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY(isLandscape)
   857	    val accountPromptTextSize = signInAccountPromptTextSize(isLandscape)
   858	    val accountPromptSecondaryTextSize = accountPromptTextSize * ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SECONDARY_TEXT_SCALE
   859	    Box(
   860	        modifier = Modifier
   861	            .requiredSize(figma(1194f, scale), figma(834f, scale))
   862	            .graphicsLayer {
   863	                this.alpha = alpha
   864	                translationY = yOffset * scale * density
   865	            }
   866	    ) {
   867	        // Figma Logo Slide 2, Property 1=Variant2, constrained inside Sign-in Frame.
   868	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 152f, y = 450f + stackShiftY, w = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, h = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.steve_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test)
   869	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 390f, y = 450f + stackShiftY, w = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, h = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.martin_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test)
   870	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 628f, y = 454f + stackShiftY, w = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, h = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.janny_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test)
   871	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 866f, y = 450f + stackShiftY, w = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, h = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.guest_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test)
   872	
   873	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 130f, y = SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY, w = 220f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.steve_name)
   874	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 368f, y = SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY, w = 220f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.martin_name)
   875	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 606f, y = SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY, w = 220f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.janny_name)
   876	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 854f, y = SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY, w = 200f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.guest_name)
   877	        SignInPromptText(
   878	            text = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT,
   879	            x = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LEFT_ANCHOR_X,
   880	            y = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TOP + stackShiftY + accountPromptExtraShiftY,
   881	            textSize = accountPromptSecondaryTextSize,
   882	            textAlign = TextAlign.Left,
   883	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
   884	            scale = scale
   885	        )
   886	        SignInPromptText(
   887	            text = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
   888	            x = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X - ACCOUNT_PROMPT_WIDTH / 2f,
   889	            y = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TOP + stackShiftY + accountPromptExtraShiftY,
   890	            textSize = accountPromptTextSize,
   891	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
   892	            scale = scale
   893	        )
   894	        SignInPromptText(
   895	            text = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT,
   896	            x = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_RIGHT_ANCHOR_X - ACCOUNT_PROMPT_WIDTH,
   897	            y = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TOP + stackShiftY + accountPromptExtraShiftY,
   898	            textSize = accountPromptSecondaryTextSize,
   899	            textAlign = TextAlign.Right,
   900	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
   901	            scale = scale
   902	        )
   903	    }
   904	}
   905	
   906	@Composable
   907	private fun SignInPromptText(
   908	    text: String,
   909	    x: Float,
   910	    y: Float,
   911	    textSize: Float,
   912	    fontWeight: FontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
   913	    textAlign: TextAlign = TextAlign.Center,
   914	    scale: Float
   915	) {
   916	    val contentAlignment = when (textAlign) {
   917	        TextAlign.Left -> Alignment.CenterStart
   918	        TextAlign.Right -> Alignment.CenterEnd
   919	        else -> Alignment.Center
   920	    }
   921	    Box(
   922	        modifier = Modifier
   923	            .absoluteOffset(
   924	                x = figma(x, scale),
   925	                y = figma(y, scale)
   926	            )
   927	            .requiredSize(
   928	                width = figma(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_WIDTH, scale),
   929	                height = figma(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_HEIGHT, scale)
   930	            ),
   931	        contentAlignment = contentAlignment
   932	    ) {
   933	        Text(
   934	            text = text,
   935	            color = Color.White,
   936	            fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
   937	            fontSize = (textSize * scale).sp,
   938	            fontWeight = fontWeight,
   939	            lineHeight = (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LINE_HEIGHT * textSize / ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * scale).sp,
   940	            maxLines = 1,
   941	            softWrap = false,
   942	            textAlign = textAlign
   943	        )
   944	    }
   945	}

   947	@Composable
   948	private fun FigmaAvatarImage(
   949	    x: Float,
   950	    y: Float,
   951	    w: Float,
   952	    h: Float,
   953	    scale: Float,
   954	    @DrawableRes resId: Int
   955	) {
   956	    Image(
   957	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
   958	        contentDescription = null,
   959	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
   960	        modifier = Modifier
   961	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(x, scale), y = figma(y, scale))
   962	            .requiredSize(figma(w, scale), figma(h, scale))
   963	            .clip(CircleShape)
   964	    )
   965	}

   973	            .clip(CircleShape)
   974	            .background(if (active) Color(0xFFE7E7E7) else Color(0xFF050505))
   975	    )
   976	}

   978	@Composable
   979	private fun PromoBackgroundLayer(
   980	    scale: Float,
   981	    darkOverlay: Float,
   982	    @DrawableRes backgroundResId: Int,
   983	    alpha: Float = 1f
   984	) {
   985	    // Nodes 793:7049 / 793:7050 at x:0 y:0 size:1584x1584.
   986	    FigmaAssetImage(
   987	        x = 0f,
   988	        y = 0f,
   989	        w = 1584f,
   990	        h = 1584f,
   991	        scale = scale,
   992	        resId = backgroundResId,
   993	        alpha = alpha
   994	    )
   995	
   996	    if (darkOverlay > 0f) {
   997	        Box(
   998	            modifier = Modifier
   999	                .absoluteOffset(x = figma(0f, scale), y = figma(0f, scale))
  1000	                .requiredSize(figma(1584f, scale), figma(1584f, scale))
  1001	                .graphicsLayer(alpha = alpha)
  1002	                .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = darkOverlay))
  1003	        )
  1004	    }
  1005	}
  1006	
  1007	@Composable
  1008	private fun TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
  1009	    base: FigmaBounds,
  1010	    target: FigmaBounds,
  1011	    scale: Float,
  1012	    @DrawableRes resId: Int,
  1013	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1014	) {
  1015	    val density = LocalDensity.current.density
  1016	    Image(
  1017	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
  1018	        contentDescription = null,
  1019	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
  1020	        modifier = Modifier
  1021	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(base.x, scale), y = figma(base.y, scale))
  1022	            .requiredSize(figma(base.w, scale), figma(base.h, scale))
  1023	            .graphicsLayer {
  1024	                this.alpha = alpha
  1025	                transformOrigin = TransformOrigin(0f, 0f)
  1026	                translationX = (target.x - base.x) * scale * density
  1027	                translationY = (target.y - base.y) * scale * density
  1028	                scaleX = target.w / base.w
  1029	                scaleY = target.h / base.h
  1030	            }
  1031	    )
  1032	}
  1033	
  1034	@Composable
  1035	private fun FigmaAssetImage(
  1036	    x: Float,
  1037	    y: Float,
  1038	    w: Float,
  1039	    h: Float,
  1040	    scale: Float,
  1041	    @DrawableRes resId: Int,
  1042	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1043	) {
  1044	    Image(
  1045	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
  1046	        contentDescription = null,
  1047	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
  1048	        modifier = Modifier
  1049	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(x, scale), y = figma(y, scale))
  1050	            .requiredSize(figma(w, scale), figma(h, scale))
  1051	            .graphicsLayer(alpha = alpha)
  1052	    )
  1053	}
  1054	
  1055	@Composable
  1056	private fun FigmaStage(background: Brush, content: @Composable BoxWithConstraintsScope.(Float) -> Unit) {
  1057	    BoxWithConstraints(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
  1058	        val outerMaxWidth = this.maxWidth
  1059	        val outerMaxHeight = this.maxHeight
  1060	        val scale = min(outerMaxWidth.value / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, outerMaxHeight.value / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  1061	        val stageWidth = figma(FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, scale)
  1062	        val stageHeight = figma(FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT, scale)
  1063	
  1064	        Box(
  1065	            modifier = Modifier
  1066	                .fillMaxSize()
  1067	                .background(background)
  1068	        ) {
  1069	            BoxWithConstraints(
  1070	                modifier = Modifier
  1071	                    .align(Alignment.Center)
  1072	                    .requiredSize(stageWidth, stageHeight)
  1073	            ) {
  1074	                content(scale)
  1075	            }
  1076	        }
  1077	    }
  1078	}
  1079	
  1080	private fun figma(px: Float, scale: Float) = (px * scale).dp
  1081	
  1082	private fun signInStackShiftY(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1083	    return if (isLandscape) {
  1084	        SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_STACK_SHIFT_Y
  1085	    } else {
  1086	        SIGN_IN_PORTRAIT_STACK_SHIFT_Y
  1087	    }
  1088	}
  1089	
  1090	private fun signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1091	    return if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
  1092	}
  1093	
  1094	private fun signInAccountPromptTextSize(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1095	    return if (isLandscape) ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LANDSCAPE_TEXT_SIZE else ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE
  1096	}
  1097	
  1098	private fun introBackgroundBrush(solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float): Brush {
  1099	    val solid = lerpColor(ColorFrame1Background, ColorFrame2Background, solidProgress)
  1100	    return Brush.verticalGradient(
  1101	        colors = listOf(
  1102	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientTop, gradientProgress),
  1103	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientCenter, gradientProgress),
  1104	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientBottom, gradientProgress)
  1105	        )
  1106	    )
  1107	}
  1108	
  1109	private fun solidBrush(color: Color): Brush = Brush.verticalGradient(
  1110	    colors = listOf(color, color)
  1111	)
  1112	
  1113	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
  1114	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
  1115	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1116	}
  1117	
  1118	private fun easedSegment(value: Float, start: Float, end: Float): Float {
  1119	    val progress = ((value - start) / (end - start)).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1120	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(progress)
  1121	}
  1122	
  1123	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1124	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
  1125	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1126	}
  1127	
  1128	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1129	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  1130	}
  1131	
  1132	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1133	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  1134	}
  1135	
  1136	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
  1137	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1138	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
  1139	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
  1140	    return base + bounce
  1141	}
  1142	
  1143	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
  1144	
  1145	private fun lerpBounds(start: FigmaBounds, end: FigmaBounds, amount: Float) = FigmaBounds(
  1146	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
  1147	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
  1148	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
  1149	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
  1150	)

   383	    override fun onSizeChanged(width: Int, height: Int, oldWidth: Int, oldHeight: Int) {
   384	        super.onSizeChanged(width, height, oldWidth, oldHeight)
   385	        pressedAvatarProfile = null
   386	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   387	        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   388	        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   389	        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   390	        pressedMockField = null
   391	        pressedRememberMe = false
   392	        pressedBackChevron = false
   393	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   394	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   395	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   396	        pressedMockDropdown = null
   397	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   398	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   399	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   400	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   401	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   402	        mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   403	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()

   410	
   411	    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
   412	        super.onDraw(canvas)
   413	
   414	        val progress = bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis)
   415	        val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
   416	            progress,
   417	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
   418	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
   419	        )
   420	        val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2500, 3900)
   421	        drawIntroBackground(canvas, blackToPurple, purpleToSettle)
   422	
   423	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return
   424	        finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded()
   425	        finishMockCreateAvatarCarouselIfNeeded()
   426	        finishMockForgotPasswordSubmissionIfNeeded()
   427	        val mockProgress = mockSignInProgress()
   428	        updateMockFormFocusAnimation()
   429	        updateMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   430	        updateMockInputDimAnimation()
   431	        val formFocusMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress)
   432	
   433	        val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(progress, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
   434	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
   435	            drawSettlingLogo(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, progress, logoAlpha, formFocusMotion)
   436	        }
   437	
   438	        val avatarAlpha = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   439	        val mockMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockProgress)
   440	        if (avatarAlpha > 0.01f) {
   441	            val stackAlpha = avatarAlpha * (1f - mockMotion)
   442	            val y = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha) + MOCK_SIGN_IN_STACK_EXIT_Y * mockMotion
   443	            val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
   444	            if (stackAlpha > 0.01f) {
   445	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveAvatar, Bounds(152f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   446	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinAvatar, Bounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   447	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyAvatar, Bounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   448	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestAvatar, Bounds(866f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   449	
   450	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveName, Bounds(130f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   451	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinName, Bounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   452	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyName, Bounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   453	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestName, Bounds(854f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 200f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   454	                drawAccountPrompt(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, y, stackAlpha)
   455	            }
   456	        }
   457	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   458	            drawMockSignInForm(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, mockMotion, formFocusMotion)
   459	        }
   460	
   461	        if (
   462	            progress < 1f ||
   463	            isMockSignInAnimating() ||
   464	            isMockFormLabelAnimating() ||
   465	            isMockFormFocusAnimating() ||
   466	            isMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimating() ||
   467	            isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating() ||
   468	            isMockForgotPasswordSubmissionAnimating() ||
   469	            isMockInputDimAnimating()
   470	        ) {
   471	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   472	        }
   473	    }
   474	
   475	    override fun onTouchEvent(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
   476	        return when (event.actionMasked) {
   477	            MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
   478	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   479	                    mockTouchDownX = event.x
   480	                    mockTouchDownY = event.y
   481	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   482	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   483	                    val touchedDropdownOptions = mockDropdownOptionsHit(event.x, event.y)
   484	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = touchedDropdownOptions?.takeIf { mockDropdownCanScroll(it) }
   485	                    mockDropdownScrollStartY = event.y
   486	                    mockDropdownScrollStartOffset = activeMockDropdownScroll?.let { mockDropdownScrollOffset(it) } ?: 0
   487	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = if (touchedDropdownOptions != null) {
   488	                        mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   489	                    } else {
   490	                        null
   491	                    }
   492	                    pressedMockDropdown = if (pressedMockDropdownOption == null && touchedDropdownOptions == null) {
   493	                        mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   494	                    } else {
   495	                        null
   496	                    }
   497	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = if (
   498	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   499	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   500	                        pressedMockDropdown == null
   501	                    ) {
   502	                        mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   503	                    } else {
   504	                        null
   505	                    }
   506	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   507	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   508	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   509	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   510	                        mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   511	                    val touchedCreateAvatarCarousel = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   512	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   513	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   514	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   515	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   516	                        mockCreateAvatarCarouselHit(event.x, event.y) &&
   517	                        !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
   518	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   519	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   520	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   521	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = if (
   522	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   523	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   524	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   525	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   526	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   527	                        touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   528	                    ) {
   529	                        mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   530	                    } else {
   531	                        null
   532	                    }
   533	                    pressedMockField = if (
   534	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   535	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   536	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   537	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   538	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   539	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   540	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   541	                    ) {
   542	                        mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   543	                    } else {
   544	                        null
   545	                    }
   546	                    pressedRememberMe = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   547	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   548	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   549	                        pressedMockField == null &&
   550	                        mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   551	                    pressedBackChevron = pressedMockField == null &&
   552	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   553	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   554	                        !pressedRememberMe &&
   555	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   556	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   557	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   558	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   559	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   560	                        mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   561	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   562	                    return true
   563	                }
   564	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   565	                pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   566	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   567	                    !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   568	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   569	                pressedAvatarProfile = if (
   570	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   571	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   572	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt
   573	                ) {
   574	                    null
   575	                } else {
   576	                    settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
   577	                }
   578	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   579	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   580	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   581	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null
   582	            }
   583	            MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
   584	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   585	                    if (updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event)) {
   586	                        return true
   587	                    }
   588	                    if (updateMockDropdownScroll(event)) {
   589	                        return true
   590	                    }
   591	                    if (mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) == null && shouldStartMockDragReturn(event)) {
   592	                        mockDragReturnInProgress = true
   593	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
   594	                    }
   595	                    return true
   596	                }
   597	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   598	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   599	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   600	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null
   601	            }
   602	            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
   603	                if (mockDragReturnInProgress) {
   604	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   605	                    pressedMockField = null
   606	                    pressedRememberMe = false
   607	                    pressedBackChevron = false
   608	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   609	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   610	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   611	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   612	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   613	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   614	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   615	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   616	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   617	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   618	                    return true
   619	                }
   620	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   621	                    val releasedDropdownOption = mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   622	                    val releasedDropdown = mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   623	                    val releasedField = mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   624	                    val releasedRememberMe = mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   625	                    val releasedBackChevron = mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   626	                    val releasedCreateAvatarNav = mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   627	                    val releasedForgotRecoveryTarget = mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   628	                    val releasedForgotPasswordSubmit = mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   629	                    val wasTouchingDropdownOptions = activeMockDropdownScroll != null ||
   630	                        mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) != null
   631	                    val wasScrollingDropdown = mockDropdownScrollMoved
   632	                    val wasPressingRememberMe = pressedRememberMe
   633	                    val wasPressingBackChevron = pressedBackChevron
   634	                    val wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget != null
   635	                    val wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit
   636	                    val wasDraggingCreateAvatar = activeMockCreateAvatarDrag
   637	                    val createAvatarDragMoved = mockCreateAvatarDragMoved
   638	                    val createAvatarDragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
   639	                    val createAvatarDragProgress = mockCreateAvatarDragProgress()
   640	                    val pressedDropdown = pressedMockDropdown
   641	                    val pressedOption = pressedMockDropdownOption
   642	                    val pressedCreateAvatarNav = pressedMockCreateAvatarNav
   643	                    val pressedForgotRecoverySelection = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget
   644	                    val shouldSelectDropdownOption = !wasScrollingDropdown &&
   645	                        pressedOption != null &&
   646	                        pressedOption == releasedDropdownOption
   647	                    val shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null &&
   648	                        pressedForgotRecoverySelection == releasedForgotRecoveryTarget
   649	                    val shouldToggleDropdown = pressedDropdown != null && pressedDropdown == releasedDropdown
   650	                    val shouldToggleRememberMe = wasPressingRememberMe && releasedRememberMe
   651	                    val shouldCloseMockSignIn = wasPressingBackChevron && releasedBackChevron
   652	                    val shouldSubmitForgotPassword = wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit && releasedForgotPasswordSubmit
   653	                    val shouldNavigateCreateAvatar = pressedCreateAvatarNav != null &&
   654	                        !createAvatarDragMoved &&
   655	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == releasedCreateAvatarNav
   656	                    val shouldSwipeCreateAvatar = wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
   657	                        createAvatarDragMoved &&
   658	                        createAvatarDragDirection != null &&
   659	                        createAvatarDragProgress >= MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_DRAG_COMMIT_PROGRESS
   660	                    val shouldFocusField = releasedField != null &&
   661	                        !wasTouchingDropdownOptions &&
   662	                        !wasPressingRememberMe &&
   663	                        !wasPressingBackChevron &&
   664	                        !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget &&
   665	                        !wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   666	                        !wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
   667	                        pressedDropdown == null &&
   668	                        pressedOption == null &&
   669	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   670	                        (pressedMockField == null || pressedMockField == releasedField)
   671	                    pressedMockField = null
   672	                    pressedRememberMe = false
   673	                    pressedBackChevron = false
   674	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   675	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   676	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   677	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   678	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   679	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   680	                    this.pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   681	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   682	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   683	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   684	                    super.performClick()
   685	                    if (shouldCloseMockSignIn) {
   686	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
   687	                    } else if (shouldSwipeCreateAvatar && createAvatarDragDirection != null) {
   688	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   689	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   690	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(createAvatarDragDirection, createAvatarDragProgress)
   691	                    } else if (shouldNavigateCreateAvatar && pressedCreateAvatarNav != null) {
   692	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   693	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   694	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(pressedCreateAvatarNav)
   695	                    } else if (wasDraggingCreateAvatar) {
   696	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   697	                    } else if (shouldSelectDropdownOption && pressedOption != null) {
   698	                        setMockDropdownValue(pressedOption)
   699	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   700	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   701	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   702	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   703	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   704	                    } else if (shouldToggleDropdown && pressedDropdown != null) {
   705	                        expandedMockDropdown = if (expandedMockDropdown == pressedDropdown) null else pressedDropdown
   706	                        expandedMockDropdown?.let { prepareMockDropdownScroll(it) }
   707	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   708	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   709	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   710	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   711	                    } else if (shouldToggleRememberMe) {
   712	                        rememberMeChecked = !rememberMeChecked
   713	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   714	                    } else if (shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget && pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null) {
   715	                        setMockForgotRecoveryTarget(pressedForgotRecoverySelection)
   716	                    } else if (shouldSubmitForgotPassword) {
   717	                        startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
   718	                    } else if (shouldFocusField && releasedField != null) {
   719	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   720	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   721	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   722	                        focusMockSignInField(releasedField)
   723	                    } else if (wasTouchingDropdownOptions || wasScrollingDropdown) {
   724	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   725	                    } else if (!wasPressingRememberMe && !wasPressingBackChevron && !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget) {
   726	                        if (expandedMockDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
   727	                            expandedMockDropdown = null
   728	                            restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   729	                            restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   730	                        }
   731	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   732	                    } else {
   733	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   734	                    }
   735	                    return true
   736	                }
   737	                val shouldOpenMockCreateAccount = pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   738	                    settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   739	                val shouldOpenMockSignIn = pressedSignInPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   740	                val shouldOpenMockForgotPassword = pressedForgotPasswordPrompt &&
   741	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   742	                val releasedAvatarProfile = settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
   743	                val shouldNavigate = pressedAvatarProfile != null && pressedAvatarProfile == releasedAvatarProfile
   744	                clickAvatarProfile = pressedAvatarProfile
   745	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
   746	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   747	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
   748	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   749	                if (shouldOpenMockCreateAccount) {
   750	                    super.performClick()
   751	                    openMockCreateAccountScreen()
   752	                    true
   753	                } else if (shouldOpenMockSignIn) {
   754	                    super.performClick()
   755	                    openMockSignInScreen()
   756	                    true
   757	                } else if (shouldOpenMockForgotPassword) {
   758	                    super.performClick()
   759	                    openMockForgotPasswordScreen()
   760	                    true
   761	                } else if (shouldNavigate) {
   762	                    performClick()
   763	                    true
   764	                } else {
   765	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
   766	                    false
   767	                }
   768	            }
   769	            MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
   770	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
   771	                clickAvatarProfile = null
   772	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   773	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
   774	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   775	                pressedMockField = null
   776	                pressedRememberMe = false
   777	                pressedBackChevron = false
   778	                pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   779	                activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   780	                mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   781	                mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   782	                pressedMockDropdown = null
   783	                pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   784	                pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   785	                pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   786	                activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   787	                mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   788	                mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   789	                false
   790	            }
   791	            else -> pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   792	                pressedSignInPrompt ||
   793	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   794	                pressedAvatarProfile != null ||
   795	                mockSignInStartMillis != null
   796	        }
   797	    }
   798	
   799	    override fun onCheckIsTextEditor(): Boolean {
   800	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && !isMockSignInClosing()
   801	    }
   802	
   803	    override fun onCreateInputConnection(outAttrs: EditorInfo): InputConnection? {
   804	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return null
   805	        outAttrs.inputType = when (field) {
   806	            MockSignInField.Password,
   807	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_PASSWORD
   808	            MockSignInField.Email -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_EMAIL_ADDRESS
   809	            MockSignInField.Month,
   810	            MockSignInField.Day,
   811	            MockSignInField.Year -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_NUMBER
   812	            else -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_NORMAL
   813	        }
   814	        outAttrs.imeOptions = if (field == activeMockFields().lastOrNull()) {
   815	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE
   816	        } else {
   817	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_NEXT
   818	        }
   819	        val textLength = mockSignInFieldText(field).length
   820	        outAttrs.initialSelStart = textLength
   821	        outAttrs.initialSelEnd = textLength
   822	        return MockSignInInputConnection()
   823	    }
   824	
   825	    override fun onKeyDown(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
   826	        if (focusedMockField != null) {
   827	            return handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode, event) || super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
   828	        }
   829	        return super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
   830	    }
   831	
   832	    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
   833	    override fun onKeyMultiple(keyCode: Int, repeatCount: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
   834	        val characters = event.getCharacters()
   835	        if (focusedMockField != null && !characters.isNullOrEmpty()) {
   836	            return appendMockSignInInput(characters)
   837	        }
   838	        return super.onKeyMultiple(keyCode, repeatCount, event)
   839	    }
   840	
   841	    override fun performClick(): Boolean {
   842	        super.performClick()
   843	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return false
   844	        val profile = clickAvatarProfile ?: return false
   845	        onAvatarSelected?.invoke(profile)
   846	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   847	        return true
   848	    }
   849	
   850	    private fun drawSettlingLogo(
   851	        canvas: Canvas,
   852	        stageLeft: Float,
   853	        stageTop: Float,
   854	        stageScale: Float,
   855	        progress: Float,
   856	        alpha: Float,
   857	        formFocusMotion: Float
   858	    ) {
   859	        val logoEntry = bouncySegmentMs(progress, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
   860	        val finalProgress = easedSegmentMs(progress, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   861	        val focusShiftY = mockLogoFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
   862	        val base = offsetBounds(Bounds(222f, 150f, 750f, 535f), focusShiftY)
   863	        val final = offsetBounds(Bounds(297f, LOGO_FINAL_TOP, 600f, LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT), focusShiftY)
   864	
   865	        if (logoEntry >= 0.999f) {
   866	            drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoCombined, lerpBounds(base, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
   867	            return
   868	        }
   869	
   870	        val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(447f, 1037f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
   871	        val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(453f, -417f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
   872	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoSimple, lerpBounds(simpleEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
   873	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoEyes, lerpBounds(eyesEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
   874	    }
   875	
   876	    private fun drawIntroBackground(canvas: Canvas, solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float) {
   877	        val standardSignInBackground = standardSignInBackgroundForSize()
   878	        if (gradientProgress <= 0.001f) {
   879	            canvas.drawColor(lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress))
   880	            return
   881	        }
   882	
   883	        val solid = lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress)
   884	        backgroundPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
   885	            0f,
   886	            0f,
   887	            0f,
   888	            height.toFloat(),
   889	            intArrayOf(
   890	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_TOP, gradientProgress),
   891	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_CENTER, gradientProgress),
   892	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_BOTTOM, gradientProgress)
   893	            ),
   894	            null,
   895	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
   896	        )
   897	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
   898	        backgroundPaint.shader = null
   899	
   900	        if (standardSignInBackground != null) {
   901	            drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas, gradientProgress)
   902	            drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas, standardSignInBackground, gradientProgress)
   903	        }
   904	    }
   905	
   906	    private fun drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas: Canvas, alpha: Float) {
   907	        backgroundPaint.color = COLOR_FRAME_1
   908	        backgroundPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
   909	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
   910	        backgroundPaint.alpha = 255
   911	    }
   912	
   913	    private fun drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas: Canvas, bitmap: Bitmap, alpha: Float) {
   914	        tempRect.set(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat())
   915	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * STANDARD_BACKGROUND_SETTLED_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
   916	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
   917	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
   918	    }
   919	
   920	    private fun drawFigmaBitmap(
   921	        canvas: Canvas,
   922	        bitmap: Bitmap,
   923	        bounds: Bounds,
   924	        stageLeft: Float,
   925	        stageTop: Float,
   926	        stageScale: Float,
   927	        alpha: Float
   928	    ) {
   929	        tempRect.set(
   930	            stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale,
   931	            stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale,
   932	            stageLeft + (bounds.x + bounds.w) * stageScale,
   933	            stageTop + (bounds.y + bounds.h) * stageScale
   934	        )
   935	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
   936	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
   937	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
   938	    }
   939	
   940	    private fun drawAccountPrompt(
   941	        canvas: Canvas,
   942	        stageLeft: Float,
   943	        stageTop: Float,
   944	        stageScale: Float,
   945	        yOffset: Float,
   946	        alpha: Float
   947	    ) {
   948	        val isLandscape = width > height
   949	        val textSize = signInAccountPromptTextSize() * stageScale
   950	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
   951	        accountPromptPaint.textSize = textSize
   952	        formTitlePaint.textSize = textSize
   953	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
   954	        formTitlePaint.alpha = paintAlpha
   955	
   956	        val accountPromptShiftY = if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
   957	        val centerY = stageTop +
   958	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + accountPromptShiftY + yOffset) * stageScale
   959	        val secondaryMetrics = accountPromptPaint.fontMetrics
   960	        val primaryMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
   961	        val secondaryBaselineY = centerY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
   962	        val primaryBaselineY = centerY - (primaryMetrics.ascent + primaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
   963	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
   964	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
   965	            canvas.drawText(
   966	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT,
   967	                stageLeft + accountPromptCreateCenterX() * stageScale,
   968	                secondaryBaselineY,
   969	                accountPromptPaint
   970	            )
   971	        }
   972	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn) {
   973	            formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
   974	            canvas.drawText(
   975	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
   976	                stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale,
   977	                primaryBaselineY,
   978	                formTitlePaint
   979	            )
   980	        }
   981	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
   982	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
   983	            canvas.drawText(
   984	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT,
   985	                stageLeft + accountPromptForgotCenterX() * stageScale,
   986	                secondaryBaselineY,
   987	                accountPromptPaint
   988	            )
   989	        }
   990	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = 255
   991	        accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
   992	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
   993	    }

   995	    private fun drawMockSignInForm(
   996	        canvas: Canvas,
   997	        stageLeft: Float,
   998	        stageTop: Float,
   999	        stageScale: Float,
  1000	        progress: Float,
  1001	        formFocusMotion: Float
  1002	    ) {
  1003	        updateMockFormLabelAnimation()
  1004	
  1005	        val alpha = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1006	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1007	            drawMockCreateAccountForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1008	            return
  1009	        }
  1010	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  1011	            drawMockForgotPasswordForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1012	            return
  1013	        }
  1014	
  1015	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha)
  1016	        val fieldFillAlpha = (alpha * 26f).roundToInt()
  1017	        val fieldStrokeAlpha = (alpha * 185f).roundToInt()
  1018	        val fieldX = mockFormX()
  1019	        val usernameY = MOCK_FORM_Y + yOffset
  1020	        val passwordY = usernameY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_GAP
  1021	
  1022	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fieldFillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1023	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(fieldStrokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1024	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1025	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * stageScale
  1026	
  1027	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  1028	        val rememberMeY = passwordY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TOP_GAP + focusShiftY
  1029	        drawMockSignInTitle(canvas, usernameY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1030	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Username, fieldX, usernameY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1031	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Password, fieldX, passwordY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1032	        drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  1033	            canvas,
  1034	            fieldX,
  1035	            rememberMeY,
  1036	            stageLeft,
  1037	            stageTop,
  1038	            stageScale,
  1039	            alpha
  1040	        )
  1041	        drawBackChevron(
  1042	            canvas,
  1043	            fieldX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f,
  1044	            rememberMeY + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE + MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_TOP_GAP,
  1045	            stageLeft,
  1046	            stageTop,
  1047	            stageScale,
  1048	            alpha
  1049	        )
  1050	        drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1051	            canvas,
  1052	            stageLeft,
  1053	            stageTop,
  1054	            stageScale,
  1055	            alpha,
  1056	            yOffset,
  1057	            focusShiftY
  1058	        )
  1059	
  1060	        formLabelPaint.alpha = 255
  1061	        formInputPaint.alpha = 255
  1062	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1063	        formCaretPaint.alpha = 255
  1064	    }
