  1260	    private fun drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1261	        canvas: Canvas,
  1262	        stageLeft: Float,
  1263	        stageTop: Float,
  1264	        stageScale: Float,
  1265	        alpha: Float,
  1266	        yOffset: Float,
  1267	        focusShiftY: Float
  1268	    ) {
  1269	        val dimProgress = mockInputDimProgress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1270	        if (dimProgress <= 0.01f) return
  1271	
  1272	        inputDimPaint.alpha = (dimProgress * MOCK_INPUT_DIM_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1273	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), inputDimPaint)
  1274	        inputDimPaint.alpha = 255
  1275	
  1276	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 26f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1277	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 185f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1278	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1279	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * stageScale
  1280	
  1281	        val activeDropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: mockInputDimActiveDropdown
  1282	        if (activeDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1283	            drawMockDropdownControl(
  1284	                canvas,
  1285	                activeDropdown,
  1286	                mockDropdownX(activeDropdown),
  1287	                mockDropdownY(activeDropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1288	                stageLeft,
  1289	                stageTop,
  1290	                stageScale,
  1291	                alpha,
  1292	                mockDropdownWidth(activeDropdown)
  1293	            )
  1294	            if (expandedMockDropdown == activeDropdown) {
  1295	                drawMockDropdownOptions(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1296	            }
  1297	            return
  1298	        }
  1299	
  1300	        val activeField = focusedMockField ?: mockInputDimActiveField ?: return
  1301	        drawMockSignInField(
  1302	            canvas,
  1303	            activeField,
  1304	            mockActiveFieldX(activeField),
  1305	            mockActiveFieldY(activeField, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1306	            stageLeft,
  1307	            stageTop,
  1308	            stageScale,
  1309	            alpha,
  1310	            mockActiveFieldWidth(activeField)
  1311	        )
  1312	    }

  1407	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButton(
  1408	        canvas: Canvas,
  1409	        x: Float,
  1410	        y: Float,
  1411	        stageLeft: Float,
  1412	        stageTop: Float,
  1413	        stageScale: Float,
  1414	        alpha: Float
  1415	    ) {
  1416	        tempRect.set(
  1417	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  1418	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  1419	            stageLeft + (x + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH) * stageScale,
  1420	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  1421	        )
  1422	        val radius = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_RADIUS * stageScale
  1423	        val submissionState = forgotPasswordSubmissionState
  1424	        val enabled = mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  1425	        val pressed = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit && enabled
  1426	        val fillAlpha = when {
  1427	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 86f
  1428	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 72f
  1429	            pressed -> 92f
  1430	            enabled -> 68f
  1431	            else -> 18f
  1432	        }
  1433	        val strokeAlpha = when {
  1434	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 245f
  1435	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 228f
  1436	            pressed -> 238f
  1437	            enabled -> 210f
  1438	            else -> 118f
  1439	        }
  1440	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  1441	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * fillAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1442	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * strokeAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1443	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1444	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  1445	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  1446	        if (submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading) {
  1447	            drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(canvas, tempRect, radius, alpha)
  1448	        }
  1449	
  1450	        formTitlePaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1451	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1452	        val metrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1453	        val baselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  1454	        val maxTextWidth = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  1455	        val showCheck = submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  1456	        val checkSize = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_SIZE * stageScale else 0f
  1457	        val checkGap = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_GAP * stageScale else 0f
  1458	        val textMaxWidth = (maxTextWidth - checkSize - checkGap).coerceAtLeast(maxTextWidth * 0.5f)
  1459	        val buttonText = trailingFittingText(
  1460	            mockForgotPasswordButtonText(),
  1461	            if (showCheck) textMaxWidth else maxTextWidth,
  1462	            formTitlePaint
  1463	        )
  1464	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * when {
  1465	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 226f
  1466	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 255f
  1467	            enabled -> 255f
  1468	            else -> 138f
  1469	        }).roundToInt()
  1470	        formTitlePaint.alpha = textAlpha
  1471	        val textX = if (showCheck) {
  1472	            val textWidth = formTitlePaint.measureText(buttonText)
  1473	            val groupWidth = checkSize + checkGap + textWidth
  1474	            val groupLeft = tempRect.centerX() - groupWidth / 2f
  1475	            val checkLeft = groupLeft
  1476	            val checkCenterY = tempRect.centerY()
  1477	            backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  1478	            backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 2.1f * stageScale
  1479	            canvas.drawLine(
  1480	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.18f,
  1481	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.03f,
  1482	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  1483	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  1484	                backChevronPaint
  1485	            )
  1486	            canvas.drawLine(
  1487	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  1488	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  1489	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.84f,
  1490	                checkCenterY - checkSize * 0.28f,
  1491	                backChevronPaint
  1492	            )
  1493	            groupLeft + checkSize + checkGap + textWidth / 2f
  1494	        } else {
  1495	            tempRect.centerX()
  1496	        }
  1497	        canvas.drawText(
  1498	            buttonText,
  1499	            textX,
  1500	            baselineY,
  1501	            formTitlePaint
  1502	        )
  1503	    }
  1504	
  1505	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(
  1506	        canvas: Canvas,
  1507	        buttonRect: RectF,
  1508	        radius: Float,
  1509	        alpha: Float
  1510	    ) {
  1511	        val progress = mockForgotPasswordSubmissionProgress()
  1512	        val sweepWidth = buttonRect.width() * 0.44f
  1513	        val sweepCenterX = buttonRect.left + buttonRect.width() * (progress * 1.35f - 0.18f)
  1514	        val sweepLeft = sweepCenterX - sweepWidth / 2f
  1515	        val sweepRight = sweepCenterX + sweepWidth / 2f
  1516	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  1517	            sweepLeft,
  1518	            buttonRect.top,
  1519	            sweepRight,
  1520	            buttonRect.top,
  1521	            intArrayOf(
  1522	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255),
  1523	                Color.argb((alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 54f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255),
  1524	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255)
  1525	            ),
  1526	            floatArrayOf(0f, 0.5f, 1f),
  1527	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  1528	        )
  1529	        canvas.save()
  1530	        tempPath.reset()
  1531	        tempPath.addRoundRect(buttonRect, radius, radius, Path.Direction.CW)
  1532	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  1533	        canvas.drawRect(sweepLeft, buttonRect.top, sweepRight, buttonRect.bottom, formFieldFillPaint)
  1534	        canvas.restore()
  1535	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  1536	    }

  1586	    private fun drawMockForgotRecoveryOption(

  1595	        val bottom = top + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_HEIGHT * stageScale

  1597	        val selected = forgotRecoveryTarget == target
  1598	        val pressed = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == target
  1599	        val fillAlpha = (alpha * when {
  1600	            pressed -> 82f

  1602	            else -> 14f

  1612	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(strokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)

  1614	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  1615	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  1616	
  1617	        formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1618	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (selected || pressed) 255f else 178f).roundToInt()
  1619	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1620	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  1621	        canvas.drawText(
  1622	            target.displayText,

  1624	            tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  1625	            formInputPaint
  1626	        )

  1629	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordBodyParagraph(
  1630	        canvas: Canvas,
  1631	        text: String,
  1632	        centerX: Float,
  1633	        topY: Float,
  1634	        maxWidth: Float,
  1635	        stageLeft: Float,
  1636	        stageTop: Float,
  1637	        stageScale: Float,
  1638	        alpha: Float
  1639	    ): Float {
  1640	        val paragraphs = text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n"))
  1641	        val leadBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  1642	            canvas,
  1643	            paragraphs.firstOrNull().orEmpty(),
  1644	            centerX,
  1645	            topY,
  1646	            maxWidth,
  1647	            stageLeft,
  1648	            stageTop,
  1649	            stageScale,
  1650	            alpha,
  1651	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_TEXT_SIZE,
  1652	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_LINE_HEIGHT
  1653	        )
  1654	        val middleText = paragraphs.getOrNull(1) ?: return leadBottomY
  1655	        val middleBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  1656	            canvas,
  1657	            middleText,
  1658	            centerX,
  1659	            leadBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  1660	            maxWidth,
  1661	            stageLeft,
  1662	            stageTop,
  1663	            stageScale,
  1664	            alpha,
  1665	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  1666	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT
  1667	        )
  1668	        val bottomText = paragraphs.drop(2).joinToString("\n\n")
  1669	        if (bottomText.isEmpty()) return middleBottomY
  1670	        return drawMockFormParagraph(
  1671	            canvas,
  1672	            bottomText,
  1673	            centerX,
  1674	            middleBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  1675	            maxWidth,
  1676	            stageLeft,
  1677	            stageTop,
  1678	            stageScale,
  1679	            alpha,
  1680	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  1681	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT
  1682	        )
  1683	    }
  1684	
  1685	    private fun drawMockFormParagraph(
  1686	        canvas: Canvas,
  1687	        text: String,
  1688	        centerX: Float,
  1689	        topY: Float,
  1690	        maxWidth: Float,
  1691	        stageLeft: Float,
  1692	        stageTop: Float,
  1693	        stageScale: Float,
  1694	        alpha: Float,
  1695	        textSize: Float,
  1696	        lineHeight: Float
  1697	    ): Float {
  1698	        formInputPaint.textSize = textSize * stageScale
  1699	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 218f).roundToInt()
  1700	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1701	
  1702	        val lines = wrappedTextLines(text, maxWidth * stageScale, formInputPaint)
  1703	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  1704	        val x = stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  1705	        var baselineY = stageTop + topY * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  1706	        lines.forEach { line ->
  1707	            canvas.drawText(line, x, baselineY, formInputPaint)
  1708	            baselineY += lineHeight * stageScale
  1709	        }
  1710	        return topY + lines.size * lineHeight
  1711	    }
  1712	
  1713	    private fun drawMockCreateAccountAvatarText(
  1714	        canvas: Canvas,
  1715	        columnX: Float,
  1716	        firstFieldY: Float,
  1717	        stageLeft: Float,
  1718	        stageTop: Float,
  1719	        stageScale: Float,
  1720	        alpha: Float
  1721	    ) {
  1722	        val avatarScale = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE
  1723	        val scaledAvatarSize = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * avatarScale
  1724	        val labelBaselineFromTop = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LABEL_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * avatarScale
  1725	        val avatarTopGap = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_TOP_GAP * avatarScale
  1726	        val centerX = stageLeft + (columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f) * stageScale
  1727	        val baselineY = stageTop + (firstFieldY + labelBaselineFromTop) * stageScale
  1728	        formLabelPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * avatarScale * stageScale
  1729	        formLabelPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1730	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1731	        canvas.drawText(
  1732	            MOCK_FORM_AVATAR_TEXT,
  1733	            centerX,
  1734	            baselineY,
  1735	            formLabelPaint
  1736	        )
  1737	
  1738	        val avatarLeftX = columnX + (MOCK_FORM_WIDTH - scaledAvatarSize) / 2f
  1739	        val avatarTopY = firstFieldY + labelBaselineFromTop + avatarTopGap
  1740	        val avatarBounds = Bounds(
  1741	            avatarLeftX,
  1742	            avatarTopY,
  1743	            scaledAvatarSize,
  1744	            scaledAvatarSize
  1745	        )
  1746	        val carouselProgress = mockCreateAvatarCarouselProgress()
  1747	        val slideDirection = mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection.stageDirection
  1748	        val slideDistance = mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(scaledAvatarSize)
  1749	        val dragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
  1750	        val dragProgress = mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(slideDistance, stageScale)
  1751	
  1752	        drawMockCreateAvatarBubbleShell(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1753	
  1754	        canvas.save()
  1755	        clipMockCreateAvatarBubbleInterior(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale)
  1756	        if (
  1757	            activeMockCreateAvatarDrag &&
  1758	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  1759	            dragDirection != null &&
  1760	            !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
  1761	        ) {
  1762	            val dragSlideDirection = dragDirection.stageDirection
  1763	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  1764	                canvas,
  1765	                mockCreateAvatarIndex,
  1766	                avatarBounds,
  1767	                -dragSlideDirection * dragProgress * slideDistance,
  1768	                stageLeft,
  1769	                stageTop,
  1770	                stageScale,
  1771	                alpha
  1772	            )
  1773	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  1774	                canvas,
  1775	                mockCreateAvatarIndexForDirection(dragDirection),
  1776	                avatarBounds,
  1777	                dragSlideDirection * (1f - dragProgress) * slideDistance,
  1778	                stageLeft,
  1779	                stageTop,
  1780	                stageScale,
  1781	                alpha
  1782	            )
  1783	        } else if (isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()) {
  1784	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  1785	                canvas,
  1786	                mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex,
  1787	                avatarBounds,
  1788	                -slideDirection * carouselProgress * slideDistance,
  1789	                stageLeft,
  1790	                stageTop,
  1791	                stageScale,
  1792	                alpha
  1793	            )
  1794	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  1795	                canvas,
  1796	                mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex,
  1797	                avatarBounds,
  1798	                slideDirection * (1f - carouselProgress) * slideDistance,
  1799	                stageLeft,
  1800	                stageTop,
  1801	                stageScale,
  1802	                alpha
  1803	            )
  1804	        } else {
  1805	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  1806	                canvas,
  1807	                mockCreateAvatarIndex,
  1808	                avatarBounds,
  1809	                0f,
  1810	                stageLeft,
  1811	                stageTop,
  1812	                stageScale,
  1813	                alpha
  1814	            )
  1815	        }
  1816	        canvas.restore()
  1817	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  1818	            canvas,
  1819	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous,
  1820	            avatarBounds,
  1821	            stageLeft,
  1822	            stageTop,
  1823	            stageScale,
  1824	            alpha
  1825	        )
  1826	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  1827	            canvas,
  1828	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next,
  1829	            avatarBounds,
  1830	            stageLeft,
  1831	            stageTop,
  1832	            stageScale,
  1833	            alpha
  1834	        )
  1835	    }

  1877	    private fun clipMockCreateAvatarBubbleInterior(
  1878	        canvas: Canvas,
  1879	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  1880	        stageLeft: Float,
  1881	        stageTop: Float,
  1882	        stageScale: Float
  1883	    ) {
  1884	        tempPath.reset()
  1885	        tempPath.addCircle(
  1886	            stageLeft + (avatarBounds.x + avatarBounds.w / 2f) * stageScale,
  1887	            stageTop + (avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h / 2f) * stageScale,
  1888	            mockCreateAvatarBubbleInnerRadius(avatarBounds, stageScale),
  1889	            Path.Direction.CW
  1890	        )
  1891	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  1892	    }

  1894	    private fun mockCreateAvatarBubbleInnerRadius(avatarBounds: Bounds, stageScale: Float): Float {
  1895	        return (avatarBounds.w / 2f - avatarBounds.w * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO) * stageScale
  1896	    }

  1898	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  1899	        canvas: Canvas,
  1900	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  1901	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  1902	        stageLeft: Float,
  1903	        stageTop: Float,
  1904	        stageScale: Float,
  1905	        alpha: Float
  1906	    ) {
  1907	        val centerX = stageLeft + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * stageScale
  1908	        val centerY = stageTop + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterY(avatarBounds) * stageScale
  1909	        val halfWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  1910	        val halfHeight = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * stageScale / 2f
  1911	        val pressedAlpha = if (pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == direction) 0.62f else 0.9f
  1912	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * pressedAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  1913	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1914	
  1915	        if (direction == MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous) {
  1916	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX - halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  1917	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY, centerX + halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  1918	        } else {
  1919	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX + halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  1920	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY, centerX - halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  1921	        }
  1922	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  1923	    }

  1925	    private fun drawMockDropdownControl(
  1926	        canvas: Canvas,
  1927	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  1928	        x: Float,
  1929	        y: Float,
  1930	        stageLeft: Float,
  1931	        stageTop: Float,
  1932	        stageScale: Float,
  1933	        alpha: Float,
  1934	        dropdownWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  1935	    ) {
  1936	        tempRect.set(
  1937	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  1938	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  1939	            stageLeft + (x + dropdownWidth) * stageScale,
  1940	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  1941	        )
  1942	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  1943	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  1944	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  1945	
  1946	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1947	        formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  1948	        formInputPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  1949	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  1950	        val textInset = mockDropdownTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  1951	        val chevronInset = mockDropdownChevronInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  1952	        val maxTextWidth = (dropdownWidth * stageScale - textInset - chevronInset * 2f).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  1953	        canvas.drawText(
  1954	            trailingFittingText(mockDropdownDisplayText(dropdown), maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  1955	            tempRect.left + textInset,
  1956	            tempRect.centerY() - (formInputPaint.fontMetrics.ascent + formInputPaint.fontMetrics.descent) / 2f,
  1957	            formInputPaint
  1958	        )
  1959	
  1960	        val centerX = tempRect.right - chevronInset
  1961	        val centerY = tempRect.centerY() + 2f * stageScale
  1962	        val halfSize = mockDropdownChevronSize(dropdown) * stageScale / 2f
  1963	        backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  1964	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 1.65f * stageScale
  1965	        canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  1966	        canvas.drawLine(centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, centerX + halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  1967	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  1968	    }

  1970	    private fun drawMockDropdownOptions(
  1971	        canvas: Canvas,
  1972	        stageLeft: Float,
  1973	        stageTop: Float,
  1974	        stageScale: Float,
  1975	        alpha: Float
  1976	    ) {
  1977	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return
  1978	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return
  1979	
  1980	        val x = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  1981	        val y = mockDropdownOptionsY(
  1982	            dropdown,
  1983	            MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha),
  1984	            mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  1985	        )
  1986	        val left = stageLeft + x * stageScale
  1987	        val top = stageTop + y * stageScale
  1988	        val right = stageLeft + (x + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stageScale
  1989	        val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stageScale
  1990	        val optionAlpha = 255
  1991	        val optionTextInset = mockDropdownOptionTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  1992	        val scrollbarReservedWidth = if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  1993	            (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stageScale
  1994	        } else {
  1995	            0f
  1996	        }
  1997	        val maxTextWidth = (right - left - optionTextInset - scrollbarReservedWidth).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  1998	
  1999	        val visibleOptions = mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown)
  2000	        visibleOptions.forEachIndexed { index, option ->
  2001	            val optionTop = top + index * optionHeight
  2002	            val optionBottom = optionTop + optionHeight
  2003	            val fillColor = if (option == mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown)) {
  2004	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 76, 12, 94)
  2005	            } else {
  2006	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 14, 2, 18)
  2007	            }
  2008	            tempRect.set(left, optionTop, right, optionBottom)
  2009	            formFieldFillPaint.color = fillColor
  2010	            formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(optionAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2011	            formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = 1.2f * stageScale
  2012	            canvas.drawRect(tempRect, formFieldFillPaint)
  2013	            canvas.drawRect(tempRect, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2014	
  2015	            formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownOptionTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  2016	            formInputPaint.alpha = optionAlpha
  2017	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2018	            val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2019	            canvas.drawText(
  2020	                trailingFittingText(option, maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  2021	                left + optionTextInset,
  2022	                (optionTop + optionBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  2023	                formInputPaint
  2024	            )
  2025	        }
  2026	
  2027	        if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  2028	            drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  2029	                canvas,
  2030	                dropdown,
  2031	                top,
  2032	                right,
  2033	                optionHeight * visibleOptions.size,
  2034	                stageScale,
  2035	                optionAlpha
  2036	            )
  2037	        }
  2038	    }

  2040	    private fun drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  2041	        canvas: Canvas,
  2042	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  2043	        top: Float,
  2044	        right: Float,
  2045	        dropdownHeight: Float,
  2046	        stageScale: Float,
  2047	        alpha: Int
  2048	    ) {
  2049	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  2050	        val visibleCount = mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  2051	        val maxOffset = mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown)
  2052	        if (options.isEmpty() || maxOffset <= 0) return
  2053	
  2054	        val trackRight = right - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * stageScale
  2055	        val trackLeft = trackRight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale
  2056	        val trackTop = top + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  2057	        val trackBottom = top + dropdownHeight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  2058	        val trackHeight = (trackBottom - trackTop).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2059	        val thumbHeight = maxOf(14f * stageScale, trackHeight * visibleCount / options.size)
  2060	            .coerceAtMost(trackHeight)
  2061	        val thumbProgress = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown) / maxOffset.toFloat()
  2062	        val thumbTop = trackTop + (trackHeight - thumbHeight) * thumbProgress
  2063	        val radius = MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2064	
  2065	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, trackTop, trackRight, trackBottom)
  2066	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.28f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2067	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2068	
  2069	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, thumbTop, trackRight, thumbTop + thumbHeight)
  2070	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.78f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2071	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2072	    }

  2074	    private fun drawMockSignInField(
  2075	        canvas: Canvas,
  2076	        field: MockSignInField,
  2077	        x: Float,
  2078	        y: Float,
  2079	        stageLeft: Float,
  2080	        stageTop: Float,
  2081	        stageScale: Float,
  2082	        alpha: Float,
  2083	        fieldWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2084	    ) {
  2085	        tempRect.set(
  2086	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  2087	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  2088	            stageLeft + (x + fieldWidth) * stageScale,
  2089	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  2090	        )
  2091	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  2092	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2093	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2094	
  2095	        val label = mockSignInFieldLabel(field)
  2096	        val labelProgress = mockSignInFieldLabelProgress(field)
  2097	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2098	        formLabelPaint.textSize = lerpFloat(
  2099	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE,
  2100	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * MOCK_FORM_FLOATING_LABEL_SCALE,
  2101	            labelProgress
  2102	        ) * stageScale
  2103	        formLabelPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2104	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2105	        val metrics = formLabelPaint.fontMetrics
  2106	        val placeholderBaselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  2107	        val floatingBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_TOP_INSET_Y * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  2108	        val labelX = lerpFloat(
  2109	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale,
  2110	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_INSET_X * stageScale,
  2111	            labelProgress
  2112	        )
  2113	        val labelBaselineY = lerpFloat(placeholderBaselineY, floatingBaselineY, labelProgress)
  2114	        canvas.drawText(
  2115	            label,
  2116	            labelX,
  2117	            labelBaselineY,
  2118	            formLabelPaint
  2119	        )
  2120	
  2121	        val fieldText = mockSignInFieldDisplayText(field)
  2122	        val shouldDrawInput = fieldText.isNotEmpty() || focusedMockField == field
  2123	        if (shouldDrawInput) {
  2124	            val inputAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * labelProgress * 255f).roundToInt()
  2125	            formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2126	            formInputPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  2127	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2128	            formCaretPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  2129	
  2130	            val inputX = tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale
  2131	            val inputBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_INPUT_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * stageScale
  2132	            val maxInputWidth = fieldWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  2133	            val visibleText = trailingFittingText(fieldText, maxInputWidth, formInputPaint)
  2134	            canvas.drawText(visibleText, inputX, inputBaselineY, formInputPaint)
  2135	
  2136	            if (focusedMockField == field) {
  2137	                val caretX = inputX + formInputPaint.measureText(visibleText) + 2f * stageScale
  2138	                val inputMetrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2139	                canvas.drawLine(
  2140	                    caretX,
  2141	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.ascent,
  2142	                    caretX,
  2143	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.descent,
  2144	                    formCaretPaint
  2145	                )
  2146	            }
  2147	        }
  2148	    }

  2150	    private fun drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  2151	        canvas: Canvas,
  2152	        x: Float,
  2153	        y: Float,
  2154	        stageLeft: Float,
  2155	        stageTop: Float,
  2156	        stageScale: Float,
  2157	        alpha: Float
  2158	    ) {
  2159	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()

  2161	        val boxTop = stageTop + y * stageScale

  2163	        val boxBottom = boxTop + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE * stageScale
  2164	        val fillAlpha = (alpha * if (rememberMeChecked || pressedRememberMe) 46f else 18f).roundToInt()
  2165	        val strokeAlpha = (alpha * if (rememberMeChecked) 220f else 168f).roundToInt()

  2173	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, rememberMeBoxStrokePaint)
  2174

  2178	            canvas.drawLine(
  2179	                boxLeft + 5.3f * stageScale,

  2192	            rememberMeCheckPaint.alpha = 255
  2193	        }
  2194	
  2195	        rememberMeTextPaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2196	        rememberMeTextPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  2197	        rememberMeTextPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2198	        val metrics = rememberMeTextPaint.fontMetrics
  2199	        val baselineY = (boxTop + boxBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  2200	        canvas.drawText(
  2201	            MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT,
  2202	            boxRight + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_LABEL_GAP * stageScale,

  2209	    private fun drawBackChevron(
  2210	        canvas: Canvas,
  2211	        centerX: Float,
  2212	        topY: Float,
  2213	        stageLeft: Float,
  2214	        stageTop: Float,
  2215	        stageScale: Float,
  2216	        alpha: Float
  2217	    ) {
  2218	        val chevronAlpha = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (pressedBackChevron) 0.68f else 0.92f
  2219	        val center = stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  2220	        val top = stageTop + topY * stageScale
  2221	        val bottom = top + MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * stageScale
  2222	        val halfWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2223	
  2224	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (chevronAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  2225	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2226	        canvas.drawLine(center - halfWidth, top, center, bottom, backChevronPaint)
  2227	        canvas.drawLine(center, bottom, center + halfWidth, top, backChevronPaint)
  2228	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2229	    }

  2231	    private fun decode(resId: Int): Bitmap {
  2232	        return BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, resId, bitmapOptions)
  2233	    }
  2234	
  2235	    private fun createAvatarCharacterBitmap(
  2236	        source: Bitmap,
  2237	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean = false
  2238	    ): Bitmap {
  2239	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  2240	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  2241	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  2242	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2243	
  2244	        val mask = createAvatarCharacterMask(pixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, includeDarkSaturatedPixels)
  2245	        val outputPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  2246	            if (mask[index]) pixels[index] else Color.TRANSPARENT
  2247	        }
  2248	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  2249	            setPixels(outputPixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2250	        }
  2251	    }
  2252	
  2253	    private fun createAvatarBubbleShellBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  2254	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  2255	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  2256	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  2257	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2258	
  2259	        val centerX = (bitmapWidth - 1f) / 2f
  2260	        val centerY = (bitmapHeight - 1f) / 2f
  2261	        val innerRadius = min(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight) * (0.5f - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO)
  2262	        val innerRadiusSquared = innerRadius * innerRadius
  2263	
  2264	        val outputPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  2265	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  2266	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  2267	            val dx = x - centerX
  2268	            val dy = y - centerY
  2269	            if (dx * dx + dy * dy < innerRadiusSquared) {
  2270	                Color.TRANSPARENT
  2271	            } else {
  2272	                pixels[index]
  2273	            }
  2274	        }
  2275	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  2276	            setPixels(outputPixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2277	        }
  2278	    }
  2279	
  2280	    private fun createAvatarCharacterMask(
  2281	        pixels: IntArray,
  2282	        bitmapWidth: Int,
  2283	        bitmapHeight: Int,
  2284	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean
  2285	    ): BooleanArray {
  2286	        val coloredPixels = BooleanArray(pixels.size)
  2287	        pixels.forEachIndexed { index, pixel ->
  2288	            coloredPixels[index] = isColorfulAvatarPixel(pixel, includeDarkSaturatedPixels)
  2289	        }
  2290	
  2291	        val mask = BooleanArray(pixels.size)
  2292	        val outlineRadius = 7
  2293	        for (y in 0 until bitmapHeight) {
  2294	            for (x in 0 until bitmapWidth) {
  2295	                val index = y * bitmapWidth + x
  2296	                if (!coloredPixels[index]) continue
  2297	
  2298	                mask[index] = true
  2299	                for (dy in -outlineRadius..outlineRadius) {
  2300	                    val sampleY = y + dy
  2301	                    if (sampleY !in 0 until bitmapHeight) continue
  2302	                    for (dx in -outlineRadius..outlineRadius) {
  2303	                        val sampleX = x + dx
  2304	                        if (sampleX !in 0 until bitmapWidth) continue
  2305	                        val sampleIndex = sampleY * bitmapWidth + sampleX
  2306	                        val samplePixel = pixels[sampleIndex]
  2307	                        val sampleAlpha = Color.alpha(samplePixel)
  2308	                        val sampleMax = maxOf(
  2309	                            Color.red(samplePixel),
  2310	                            Color.green(samplePixel),
  2311	                            Color.blue(samplePixel)
  2312	                        )
  2313	                        if (sampleAlpha > 16 && sampleMax <= 44) {
  2314	                            mask[sampleIndex] = true
  2315	                        }
  2316	                    }
  2317	                }
  2318	            }
  2319	        }
  2320	        return mask
  2321	    }
  2322	
  2323	    private fun isColorfulAvatarPixel(
  2324	        pixel: Int,
  2325	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean
  2326	    ): Boolean {
  2327	        val alpha = Color.alpha(pixel)
  2328	        val red = Color.red(pixel)
  2329	        val green = Color.green(pixel)
  2330	        val blue = Color.blue(pixel)
  2331	        val channelMax = maxOf(red, green, blue)
  2332	        val channelMin = minOf(red, green, blue)
  2333	        if (alpha <= 16) return false
  2334	
  2335	        val chroma = channelMax - channelMin
  2336	        return (channelMax > 48 && chroma > 22) ||
  2337	            (includeDarkSaturatedPixels && channelMax > 36 && chroma > 14)
  2338	    }
  2339	
  2340	    private fun standardSignInBackgroundForSize(): Bitmap? {
  2341	        return when {
  2342	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) &&
  2343	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) -> standardLandscapeBackground
  2344	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) &&
  2345	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) -> standardPortraitBackground
  2346	            else -> null
  2347	        }
  2348	    }
  2349	
  2350	    private fun isSizeCloseTo(actual: Int, expected: Int): Boolean {
  2351	        return abs(actual - expected) <= STANDARD_TABLET_SIZE_TOLERANCE
  2352	    }

  2355	        return bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis) >= 1f
  2356	    }

  2358	    private fun settledAvatarHitProfile(x: Float, y: Float): CinerificProfile? {
  2359	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) return null
  2360	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return null
  2361	
  2362	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  2363	
  2364	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
  2365	        return FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.firstOrNull { target ->
  2366	            val bounds = target.bounds
  2367	            val centerX = stage.left + (bounds.x + bounds.w / 2f) * stage.scale
  2368	            val centerY = stage.top + (bounds.y + stackShiftY + bounds.h / 2f) * stage.scale
  2369	            val radius = min(bounds.w, bounds.h) * stage.scale / 2f
  2370	            val dx = x - centerX
  2371	            val dy = y - centerY
  2372	            dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius
  2373	        }?.profile
  2374	    }

  2531	    private fun updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  2532	        if (!activeMockCreateAvatarDrag) return false
  2533

  2535	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockTouchDownY
  2536	        if (
  2537	            !mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  2538	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > touchSlop &&
  2539	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > abs(dragDeltaY)
  2540	        ) {
  2541	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = true
  2542	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  2543	        }
  2544	
  2545	        if (mockCreateAvatarDragMoved) {
  2546	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  2547	        }
  2548	        return true
  2549	    }

  2551	    private fun mockDropdownOptionHit(pointerX: Float, pointerY: Float): MockDropdownOption? {
  2552	        if (
  2553	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  2554	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount ||
  2555	            expandedMockDropdown == null
  2556	        ) {
  2557	            return null
  2558	        }
  2559	
  2560	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return null
  2561	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  2562	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  2563	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  2564	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  2565	        val dropdownX = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  2566	        val yStart = mockDropdownOptionsY(dropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  2567	        val left = stage.left + dropdownX * stage.scale
  2568	        val right = stage.left + (dropdownX + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stage.scale
  2569	        if (pointerX !in left..right) return null
  2570	        if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  2571	            val scrollbarLeft = right -
  2572	                (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stage.scale
  2573	            if (pointerX >= scrollbarLeft) return null
  2574	        }
  2575	
  2576	        mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown).forEachIndexed { optionIndex, option ->
  2577	            val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown)
  2578	            val top = stage.top + (yStart + optionIndex * optionHeight) * stage.scale
  2579	            val bottom = top + optionHeight * stage.scale
  2580	            if (pointerY in top..bottom) return MockDropdownOption(dropdown, option)
  2581	        }
  2582	        return null
  2583	    }

  2608	    private fun updateMockDropdownScroll(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  2609	        val dropdown = activeMockDropdownScroll ?: return false
  2610	        if (expandedMockDropdown != dropdown || !mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) return false
  2611	
  2612	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return true
  2613	        val optionHeightPx = (mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stage.scale).coerceAtLeast(1f)
  2614	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockDropdownScrollStartY
  2615	        if (abs(dragDeltaY) > touchSlop) {
  2616	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = true
  2617	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  2618	        }
  2619	
  2620	        if (mockDropdownScrollMoved) {
  2621	            val rowDelta = ((mockDropdownScrollStartY - event.y) / optionHeightPx).roundToInt()
  2622	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, mockDropdownScrollStartOffset + rowDelta)
  2623	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  2624	        }
  2625	        return true
  2626	    }

  2651	    private fun shouldStartMockDragReturn(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  2652	        if (mockDragReturnInProgress || isMockSignInClosing()) return false
  2653	
  2654	        val upwardDragY = mockTouchDownY - event.y
  2655	        if (upwardDragY <= mockBackDragThresholdPx()) return false
  2656	
  2657	        val dragX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  2658	        return upwardDragY > abs(dragX)
  2659	    }
  2660	
  2661	    private fun mockBackDragThresholdPx(): Float {
  2662	        val stageThreshold = currentStageMetrics()?.let { stage ->
  2663	            MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD * stage.scale
  2664	        } ?: MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD
  2665	        return maxOf(stageThreshold, touchSlop * 3f)
  2666	    }

  2679	        val bottom = stage.top + (fieldY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stage.scale

  2681	    }

  2718	        val bottom = stage.top + (selectorY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_HEIGHT) * stage.scale

  2737	        pointerX: Float,
  2738	        pointerY: Float,
  2739	        centerX: Float,
  2740	        topY: Float,
  2741	        stage: StageMetrics
  2742	    ): Boolean {
  2743	        val center = stage.left + centerX * stage.scale
  2744	        val hitHalfWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HIT_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  2745	        val left = center - hitHalfWidth
  2746	        val right = center + hitHalfWidth
  2747	        val top = stage.top + (topY - (MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HIT_HEIGHT - MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HEIGHT) / 2f) * stage.scale
  2748	        val bottom = top + MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HIT_HEIGHT * stage.scale
  2749	        return pointerX in left..right && pointerY in top..bottom
  2750	    }
  2751	
  2752	    private fun mockCreateAvatarBounds(columnX: Float, firstFieldY: Float): Bounds {
  2753	        val scaledAvatarSize = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE
  2754	        return Bounds(
  2755	            columnX + (MOCK_FORM_WIDTH - scaledAvatarSize) / 2f,
  2756	            firstFieldY +
  2757	                MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LABEL_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE +
  2758	                MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_TOP_GAP * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE,
  2759	            scaledAvatarSize,
  2760	            scaledAvatarSize
  2761	        )
  2762	    }

  2764	    private fun mockCreateAvatarCarouselBounds(
  2765	        columnX: Float,
  2766	        firstFieldY: Float,
  2767	        avatarBounds: Bounds
  2768	    ): Bounds {
  2769	        val centerX = columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f
  2770	        val contentTop = firstFieldY
  2771	        val contentBottom = avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h
  2772	        val contentCenterY = (contentTop + contentBottom) / 2f
  2773	        val chevronSpan = avatarBounds.w +
  2774	            (MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_SIDE_GAP + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_WIDTH / 2f) * 2f
  2775	        val side = maxOf(chevronSpan, contentBottom - contentTop)
  2776	        return Bounds(
  2777	            centerX - side / 2f,
  2778	            contentCenterY - side / 2f,
  2779	            side,
  2780	            side
  2781	        )
  2782	    }

  2788	    private fun mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(

  2790	        avatarBounds: Bounds
  2791	    ): Float {

  2793	            avatarBounds.x - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_SIDE_GAP
  2794	        } else {
  2795	            avatarBounds.x + avatarBounds.w + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_SIDE_GAP

  2797	    }

  2803	    private fun mockCreateAvatarChevronContains(
  2804	        pointerX: Float,
  2805	        pointerY: Float,
  2806	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  2807	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2808	        stage: StageMetrics
  2809	    ): Boolean {
  2810	        val centerX = stage.left + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * stage.scale
  2811	        val centerY = stage.top + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterY(avatarBounds) * stage.scale
  2812	        val halfWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  2813	        val halfHeight = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_HEIGHT * stage.scale / 2f
  2814	        return pointerX in (centerX - halfWidth)..(centerX + halfWidth) &&
  2815	            pointerY in (centerY - halfHeight)..(centerY + halfHeight)
  2816	    }

  2821	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
  2822	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  2823	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  2824	        if (focusedMockField != field) {
  2825	            activeComposingText = ""
  2826	            focusedMockField = field

  2828	            restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  2829	        }

  2831	        post {
  2832	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  2833	            inputMethodManager?.restartInput(this)
  2834	            inputMethodManager?.showSoftInput(this, InputMethodManager.SHOW_IMPLICIT)
  2835	        }
  2836	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  2837	    }

  3185	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading
  3186	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  3187	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()

  3189	
  3190	    private fun mockForgotPasswordSubmissionProgress(): Float {
  3191	        val startMillis = forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis ?: return if (
  3192	            forgotPasswordSubmissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  3193	        ) {
  3194	            1f
  3195	        } else {
  3196	            0f
  3197	        }
  3198	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  3199	        return (elapsed / MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SUBMIT_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  3200	    }

  3202	    private fun isMockForgotPasswordSubmissionAnimating(): Boolean {
  3203	        return forgotPasswordSubmissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading
  3204	    }

  3206	    private fun finishMockForgotPasswordSubmissionIfNeeded() {
  3207	        val startMillis = forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis ?: return
  3208	        if (forgotPasswordSubmissionState != MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading) {
  3209	            forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = null
  3210	            return
  3211	        }
  3212	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SUBMIT_MS) return
  3213	
  3214	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  3215	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = null
  3216	    }

  3220	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Idle
  3221	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = null
  3222	    }

  3224	    private fun startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(
  3225	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  3226	        startProgress: Float = 0f
  3227	    ) {
  3228	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount || isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()) return
  3229	
  3230	        val nextIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndexForDirection(direction)
  3231	        if (nextIndex == mockCreateAvatarIndex) return
  3232	
  3233	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  3234	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
  3235	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex = nextIndex
  3236	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection = direction
  3237	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartProgress = startProgress.coerceIn(0f, 0.96f)
  3238	        mockCreateAvatarIndex = nextIndex
  3239	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  3240	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3241	    }

  3411	    private fun isSoftKeyboardVisible(): Boolean {
  3412	        val rootInsets = ViewCompat.getRootWindowInsets(this)
  3413	        if (rootInsets != null) {
  3414	            val imeInsets = rootInsets.getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime())
  3415	            if (rootInsets.isVisible(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime()) || imeInsets.bottom > 0) return true
  3416	        }
  3417	
  3418	        val rootHeight = rootView?.height ?: 0
  3419	        if (rootHeight <= 0) return false
  3420	        getWindowVisibleDisplayFrame(tempWindowRect)
  3421	        val obscuredHeight = rootHeight - tempWindowRect.height()
  3422	        return obscuredHeight > rootHeight * 0.18f
  3423	    }

  3533	    private fun appendMockSignInInput(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  3534	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  3535	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  3536	        if (input.isEmpty()) return true
  3537	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  3538	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  3539	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  3540	        val insertedText = input.take(availableChars)
  3541	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + insertedText)
  3542	        activeComposingText = ""
  3543	        return true
  3544	    }
  3545	
  3546	    private fun replaceMockSignInComposingText(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  3547	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  3548	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  3549	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  3550	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  3551	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  3552	        val composingText = input.take(availableChars)
  3553	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + composingText)
  3554	        activeComposingText = composingText
  3555	        return true
  3556	    }

  3563	    private fun deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength: Int): Boolean {
  3564	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  3565	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  3566	        if (currentText.isEmpty()) {
  3567	            activeComposingText = ""
  3568	            return true
  3569	        }
  3570	        val deleteCount = beforeLength.coerceAtLeast(1).coerceAtMost(currentText.length)
  3571	        activeComposingText = ""
  3572	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, currentText.dropLast(deleteCount))
  3573	        return true
  3574	    }

  3576	    private fun handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  3577	        activeComposingText = ""
  3578	        val fields = activeMockFields()
  3579	        val fieldIndex = fields.indexOf(focusedMockField)
  3580	        if (fieldIndex >= 0 && fieldIndex < fields.lastIndex && actionCode != EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE) {
  3581	            focusMockSignInField(fields[fieldIndex + 1])
  3582	        } else if (
  3583	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  3584	            focusedMockField == MockSignInField.Email &&
  3585	            mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  3586	        ) {
  3587	            startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  3588	        } else {
  3589	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  3590	            inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
  3591	            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  3592	        }
  3593	        return true
  3594	    }

  3596	    private fun handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  3597	        if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  3598	        return when (keyCode) {
  3599	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  3600	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  3601	            else -> {
  3602	                val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  3603	                typedCharacter?.let(::appendMockSignInInput) ?: false
  3604	            }
  3605	        }
  3606	    }

  3608	    private fun sanitizeMockSignInInput(input: String): String {
  3609	        return buildString {
  3610	            input.forEach { character ->
  3611	                if (character != '\n' && character != '\r' && !character.isISOControl()) {
  3612	                    append(character)
  3613	                }
  3614	            }
  3615	        }
  3616	    }
  3617	
  3618	    private fun trailingFittingText(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): String {
  3619	        if (paint.measureText(text) <= maxWidth) return text
  3620	
  3621	        var startIndex = 0
  3622	        while (startIndex < text.length && paint.measureText(text.substring(startIndex)) > maxWidth) {
  3623	            startIndex += 1
  3624	        }
  3625	        return text.substring(startIndex)
  3626	    }
  3627	
  3628	    private fun wrappedTextLines(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): List<String> {
  3629	        val lines = mutableListOf<String>()
  3630	        text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n")).forEachIndexed { paragraphIndex, paragraph ->
  3631	            if (paragraphIndex > 0) {
  3632	                lines += ""
  3633	            }
  3634	
  3635	            var currentLine = ""
  3636	            paragraph.replace('\n', ' ')
  3637	                .split(Regex("\\s+"))
  3638	                .filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
  3639	                .forEach { word ->
  3640	                    val candidateLine = if (currentLine.isEmpty()) word else "$currentLine $word"
  3641	                    if (currentLine.isEmpty() || paint.measureText(candidateLine) <= maxWidth) {
  3642	                        currentLine = candidateLine
  3643	                    } else {
  3644	                        lines += currentLine
  3645	                        currentLine = word
  3646	                    }
  3647	                }
  3648	            if (currentLine.isNotEmpty()) {
  3649	                lines += currentLine
  3650	            }
  3651	        }
  3652	        return lines
  3653	    }
  3654	
  3655	    private fun moveToward(current: Float, target: Float, amount: Float): Float {
  3656	        return when {
  3657	            current < target -> (current + amount).coerceAtMost(target)
  3658	            current > target -> (current - amount).coerceAtLeast(target)
  3659	            else -> current
  3660	        }
  3661	    }
  3662	
  3663	    private fun currentStageMetrics(): StageMetrics? {
  3664	        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return null
  3665	
  3666	        val stageScale = min(width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  3667	        return StageMetrics(
  3668	            left = (width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f,
  3669	            top = (height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * stageScale) / 2f + mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale),
  3670	            scale = stageScale
  3671	        )
  3672	    }

  3774	
  3775	    private fun mockSignInProgress(): Float {
  3776	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return 0f
  3777	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  3778	        val progress = (elapsed / mockSignInTransitionDurationMs().toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  3779	        return lerpFloat(
  3780	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress,
  3781	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress,
  3782	            progress
  3783	        )
  3784	    }
  3785	
  3786	    private fun isMockSignInAnimating(): Boolean {
  3787	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return false
  3788	        return SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()
  3789	    }
  3790	
  3791	    private fun isMockSignInClosing(): Boolean {
  3792	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f
  3793	    }
  3794	
  3795	    private fun mockSignInTransitionDurationMs(): Long {
  3796	        val distance = abs(mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress - mockSignInTransitionStartProgress)
  3797	        return (MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS * distance)
  3798	            .roundToInt()
  3799	            .coerceAtLeast(1)
  3800	            .toLong()
  3801	    }
  3802	
  3803	    private fun finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded() {
  3804	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return
  3805	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()) return
  3806	
  3807	        if (mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f) {
  3808	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
  3809	            activeMockFlow = null
  3810	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
  3811	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
  3812	            pressedMockField = null
  3813	            pressedRememberMe = false
  3814	            pressedBackChevron = false
  3815	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  3816	            pressedMockDropdown = null
  3817	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  3818	            pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  3819	            pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  3820	            activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  3821	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  3822	            mockDragReturnInProgress = false
  3823	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  3824	            resetMockCreateAvatarCarousel()
  3825	            return
  3826	        }
  3827	
  3828	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
  3829	        mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
  3830	    }
  3831	
  3832	    private inner class MockSignInInputConnection : BaseInputConnection(this@CinerificIntroView, false) {
  3833	        override fun commitText(text: CharSequence?, newCursorPosition: Int): Boolean {
  3834	            return appendMockSignInInput(text?.toString().orEmpty())
  3835	        }
  3836	
  3837	        override fun setComposingText(text: CharSequence?, newCursorPosition: Int): Boolean {
  3838	            return replaceMockSignInComposingText(text?.toString().orEmpty())
  3839	        }
  3840	
  3841	        override fun finishComposingText(): Boolean {
  3842	            return finishMockSignInComposingText()
  3843	        }
  3844	
  3845	        override fun deleteSurroundingText(beforeLength: Int, afterLength: Int): Boolean {
  3846	            return deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength)
  3847	        }
  3848	
  3849	        override fun deleteSurroundingTextInCodePoints(beforeLength: Int, afterLength: Int): Boolean {
  3850	            return deleteSurroundingText(beforeLength, afterLength)
  3851	        }
  3852	
  3853	        override fun getTextBeforeCursor(n: Int, flags: Int): CharSequence {
  3854	            val text = focusedMockField?.let(::mockSignInFieldText).orEmpty()
  3855	            return text.takeLast(n.coerceAtLeast(0))
  3856	        }
  3857	
  3858	        override fun getTextAfterCursor(n: Int, flags: Int): CharSequence {
  3859	            return ""
  3860	        }
  3861	
  3862	        override fun getSelectedText(flags: Int): CharSequence {
  3863	            return ""
  3864	        }
  3865	
  3866	        override fun performEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  3867	            return handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode)
  3868	        }
  3869	
  3870	        override fun sendKeyEvent(event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  3871	            if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  3872	            return when (event.keyCode) {
  3873	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  3874	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  3875	                else -> {
  3876	                    val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  3877	                    if (typedCharacter != null) {
  3878	                        appendMockSignInInput(typedCharacter)
  3879	                    } else {
  3880	                        super.sendKeyEvent(event)
  3881	                    }
  3882	                }
  3883	            }
  3884	        }
  3885	    }
  3886	}

  4017	
  4018	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
  4019	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
  4020	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4021	}
  4022	
  4023	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  4024	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
  4025	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4026	}
  4027	
  4028	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  4029	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  4030	}
  4031	
  4032	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  4033	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  4034	}
  4035	
  4036	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
  4037	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4038	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
  4039	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
  4040	    return base + bounce
  4041	}
  4042	
  4043	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
  4044	
  4045	private fun lerpBounds(start: Bounds, end: Bounds, amount: Float) = Bounds(
  4046	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
  4047	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
  4048	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
  4049	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
  4050	)
  4051	
  4052	private fun offsetBounds(bounds: Bounds, yOffset: Float) = Bounds(
  4053	    x = bounds.x,
  4054	    y = bounds.y + yOffset,
  4055	    w = bounds.w,
  4056	    h = bounds.h
  4057	)
  4058	
  4059	private fun lerpColor(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  4060	    val t = amount.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4061	    return Color.argb(
  4062	        lerpInt(Color.alpha(start), Color.alpha(end), t),
  4063	        lerpInt(Color.red(start), Color.red(end), t),
  4064	        lerpInt(Color.green(start), Color.green(end), t),
  4065	        lerpInt(Color.blue(start), Color.blue(end), t)
  4066	    )
  4067	}
  4068	
  4069	private fun lerpInt(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  4070	    return (start + (end - start) * amount).roundToInt()
  4071	}
  4072	
  4073	private fun combineBitmaps(base: Bitmap, overlay: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  4074	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(base.width, base.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  4075	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  4076	    val paint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  4077	    canvas.drawBitmap(base, 0f, 0f, paint)
  4078	    canvas.drawBitmap(overlay, 0f, 0f, paint)
  4079	    return output
  4080	}
  4081	
  4082	private fun circularBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  4083	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(source.width, source.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  4084	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  4085	    val rect = RectF(0f, 0f, source.width.toFloat(), source.height.toFloat())
  4086	    val maskPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG)
  4087	    val imagePaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  4088	    canvas.drawOval(rect, maskPaint)
  4089	    imagePaint.xfermode = PorterDuffXfermode(PorterDuff.Mode.SRC_IN)
  4090	    canvas.drawBitmap(source, null, rect, imagePaint)
  4091	    imagePaint.xfermode = null
  4092	    return output
  4093	}

============================================================
FILE: README.md
============================================================

     1	# Cinerific
     2	
     3	Local Android project for the `prahlin/cinerific` GitHub repo.
     4	
     5	This is a first Kotlin / Jetpack Compose scaffold based on the Figma intro and sign-in frames. The current app opens as a tablet-friendly full-screen frame preview with:
     6	
     7	- Intro frame 1
     8	- Animated intro frame 2
     9	- Promo/logo intro frame 3
    10	- Sign-in/loading frame 4
    11	
    12	## Stack
    13	
    14	- Kotlin
    15	- Jetpack Compose Material 3
    16	- Android Gradle Plugin 8.5.x
    17	- Gradle 8.7
    18	
    19	## Run it
    20	
    21	Open the project in Android Studio and run `app`, or use the Gradle wrapper:
    22	
    23	```bash
    24	./gradlew :app:assembleDebug
    25	```
    26	
    27	The project pins Gradle to Android Studio's bundled JBR in `gradle.properties`, so command-line builds do not accidentally use an unsupported system JDK.
    28	
    29	From VS Code, run the default build task `Android: assemble debug`. There are also tasks for `Android: install debug` and `Android: launch Cinerific` when an emulator or device is connected.
    30	
    31	## Notes for the first Kotlin pass
    32	
    33	- The layout is intentionally simple and adaptive for tablets.
    34	- The current visuals are Compose approximations of the Figma frames and are meant to be replaced with production assets screen by screen.
    35	- The local project folder is the same path as the GitHub remote: `prahlin/cinerific`.

     4	}

============================================================
FILE: read_project.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/bin/bash
     2	
     3	PROJECT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
     4	OUTPUT_FILE="$PROJECT_DIR/cinerific_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt"
     5	
     6	echo "Generating Cinerific project snapshot..."
     7	echo "Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR"
     8	echo "Snapshot: $OUTPUT_FILE"
     9	echo ""
    10	
    11	write_header() {
    12	  cat > "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
    13	Cinerific Android PROJECT SNAPSHOT
    14	Generated: $(date)
    15	Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR
    16	
    17	IMPORTANT INSTRUCTIONS FOR CHATGPT & CODEX:
    18	1. Read this entire snapshot before giving code edits.
    19	2. Preserve all assets, spacing, alignment, fonts, images, layout, animations, and relative positioning unless specifically asked to change them.
    20	3. Do not remove unrelated code.
    21	4. Do not rewrite large sections unless requested.
    22	5. When giving code changes, include exact line/row numbers whenever possible.
    23	6. Print full scripts when requested.
    24	7. Treat this project structure as the source of truth.
    25	8. If you don't understand something, ask for clarification instead of making assumptions.
    26	9. Warn me, or at least caution me, if I ask for something that would break the project or cause it to not work as intended.
    27	
    28	
    29	============================================================
    30	SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
    31	============================================================
    32	
    33	This file is rebuilt from the current Cinerific workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
    34	Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.
    35	
    36	The snapshot intentionally ignores:
    37	- .git
    38	- .gradle
    39	- .idea
    40	- app/build
    41	- build
    42	- .kotlin
    43	- .tmp
    44	- .cache
    45	- .DS_Store
    46	- local.properties
    47	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
    48	- *.jks
    49	- *.keystore
    50	
    51	Binary image and screenshot files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.
    52	
    53	
    54	============================================================
    55	PROJECT FILE TREE
    56	============================================================
    57	
    58	EOF
    59	}
    60	
    61	print_file() {
    62	  local file="$1"
    63	
    64	  if [ -f "$file" ]; then
    65	    {
    66	      echo ""
    67	      echo ""
    68	      echo "============================================================"
    69	      echo "FILE: ${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}"
    70	      echo "============================================================"
    71	      echo ""
    72	      nl -ba "$file"
    73	    } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    74	  fi
    75	}
    76	
    77	print_binary_inventory() {
    78	  local title="$1"
    79	  local dir="$2"
    80	
    81	  {
    82	    echo ""
    83	    echo ""
    84	    echo "============================================================"
    85	    echo "$title"
    86	    echo "============================================================"
    87	    echo ""
    88	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    89	
    90	  if [ ! -d "$dir" ]; then
    91	    echo "Directory not present: ${dir#$PROJECT_DIR/}" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    92	    return
    93	  fi
    94	
    95	  find "$dir" -type f \
    96	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
    97	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
    98	    ! -name "*.jks" \
    99	    ! -name "*.keystore" \
   100	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   101	    local size
   102	    local modified
   103	    size="$(stat -f "%z" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%s" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   104	    modified="$(stat -f "%Sm" -t "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%y" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   105	    printf '%s | %s bytes | modified %s\n' "${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}" "$size" "$modified" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   106	  done
   107	}
   108	
   109	write_snapshot() {
   110	  write_header
   111	
   112	  find "$PROJECT_DIR" \
   113	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.git" -prune -o \
   114	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.gradle" -prune -o \
   115	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.idea" -prune -o \
   116	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/app/build" -prune -o \
   117	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/build" -prune -o \
   118	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.kotlin" -prune -o \
   119	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.tmp" -prune -o \
   120	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.cache" -prune -o \
   121	    -name ".DS_Store" -prune -o \
   122	    -name "local.properties" -prune -o \
   123	    -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" -prune -o \
   124	    -name "*.jks" -prune -o \
   125	    -name "*.keystore" -prune -o \
   126	    -print | sort >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   127	
   128	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   129	
   130	
   131	============================================================
   132	FILE CONTENTS — ANDROID SOURCE
   133	============================================================
   134	
   135	EOF
   136	
   137	  for file in \
   138	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml" \
   139	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/MainActivity.kt" \
   140	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificApp.kt" \
   141	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificIntroView.kt" \
   142	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/theme/Theme.kt" \
   143	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml" \
   144	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values/themes.xml" \
   145	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values-night/themes.xml" \
   146	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values-v31/themes.xml" \
   147	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/splash_transparent.xml" \
   148	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_launcher_background.xml" \
   149	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_launcher_foreground.xml" \

============================================================
NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
============================================================

The Cinerific snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private directories:
- .git
- .gradle
- .idea
- app/build
- build
- .kotlin
- .tmp
- .cache
- .DS_Store
- local.properties
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.jks
- *.keystore

The snapshot lists binary assets and screenshots by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF bytes.
